In contrast, second-order optimization provides principled curvature-aware updates that are proven to accelerate convergence, but its application in RL is limited by the computational complexity of Hessian estimation. In this work, we analyze second-order approximations for the actor update that leverage the full curvature information of the objective as much as possible. A stable approximation requires treating the action-value function as locally constant with respect to policy parameters, which does not generally hold in policy gradient methods. We show that this approximation becomes well-justified under a two-timescale actor-critic framework, where the critic evolves on a faster timescale and can be treated as quasi-stationary during actor updates. Building on this insight, we formulate a second-order actor-critic method for the discounted reward setting that leverages Hessian-vector product (HVP) computations, resulting in a computationally efficient and stable second-order update.

</details>


### [104] [DeepTokenEEG Enhancing Mild Cognitive Impairment and Alzheimers Classification via Tokenized EEG Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15009)
*Thinh Nguyen-Quang,Minh Long Ngo,Ngoc-Son Nguyen,Nguyen Thanh Vinh,Huy-Dung Han,Bui Thanh Tung,Nguyen Quang Linh,Khuong Vo,Manoj Vishwanath,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级高性能模型DeepTokenEEG，利用时空标记器从EEG信号中捕获AD生物标志物，仅需0.29M参数即可在联合数据集上达到最高100%准确率，比现有方法提升1.41-15.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG-based AD诊断面临数据可用性不足、深度学习模型准确率有限以及专家解读耗时等挑战，亟需一种轻量且高精度的自动化诊断方法。

Method: 设计DeepTokenEEG模型，包含空间和时间标记器（spatial and temporal tokenizer），从时域和频域两个维度捕获AD相关生物标志物，模型参数量仅0.29M。

Result: 在包含274名受试者（180例AD、94例健康对照）的联合数据集上，特定频段达到最高100%准确率，相比现有最优方法提升1.41-15.35%。

Conclusion: DeepTokenEEG在AD早期检测和筛查中具有潜力，因其紧凑的模型体积具备良好的部署可行性。

Abstract: The detection of Alzheimers disease (AD) is considered crucial, as timely intervention can improve patient outcomes. Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based diagnosis has been recognized as a non-invasive, accessible, and cost-effective approach for AD detection; however, it faces challenges related to data availability, accuracy of modern deep learning methods, and the time-consuming nature of expert-based interpretation. In this study, a novel lightweight and high-performance model, DeepTokenEEG, was designed for the diagnosis of AD and the classification of EEG signals from AD patients, individuals with other neurological conditions, and healthy subjects. Unlike traditional heavy-weight models, DeepTokenEEG ultilizes spatial and temporal tokenizer that effectively captures AD-related biomarkers in both temporal and frequency domain with only 0.29 million paramaters. Trained in a combined dataset of 274 subjects, including 180 AD cases, and 94 healthy controls, the proposed method achieves a maximum recorded accuracy of 100% on specific frequency bands, representing an improvement of 1.41-15.35% over state-of-the-art methods on the same dataset. These results indicate the potential of DeepTokenEEG for early detection and screening of AD, with promising applicability for deployment due to its compact size.

</details>


### [105] [Boosting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards via Randomly Selected Few-Shot Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15012)
*Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FEST算法，仅需128条演示数据即可在RLVR中取得与全量SFT数据集相当的性能，通过监督信号、在轨策略信号和衰减权重三个关键组件解决样本效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在困难问题上样本效率低下，正确rollout难以生成；先前的演示引导RLVR方法依赖大量SFT数据，获取成本高昂。

Method: FEST算法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 监督信号（supervised signal），(2) 在轨策略信号（on-policy signal），(3) 衰减权重（decaying weights）防止多轮训练过拟合，仅需从SFT数据集中随机选取128条演示。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，仅用128条演示（全量数据的极小部分）即可超越基线方法，甚至达到与使用完整SFT数据集相当的性能。

Conclusion: 少量精心设计的演示数据配合合适的训练策略（监督信号+在轨策略信号+衰减权重）可以显著提升RLVR的样本效率，降低对大规模SFT数据的依赖。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has achieved great success in developing Large Language Models (LLMs) with chain-of-thought rollouts for many tasks such as math and coding. Nevertheless, RLVR struggles with sample efficiency on difficult problems where correct rollouts are hard to generate. Prior works propose to address this issue via demonstration-guided RLVR, i.e., to conduct Supervised FineTuning (SFT) when RL fails; however, SFT often requires a lot of data, which can be expensive to acquire. In this paper, we propose FEST, a FEw-ShoT demonstration-guided RLVR algorithm. It attains compelling results with only 128 demonstrations randomly selected from an SFT dataset. We find that three components are vital for the success: supervised signal, on-policy signal, and decaying weights on the few-shot SFT dataset to prevent overfitting from multiple-epoch training. On several benchmarks, FEST outperforms baselines with magnitudes less SFT data, even matching their performance with full dataset.

</details>


### [106] [Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018)
*Kiljae Lee,Ziqi Liu,Weijing Tang,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义优先级感知Shapley值（GPASV），将传统Shapley值扩展至任意有向加权优先级图（允许环），通过边惩罚而非禁止顺序违反来处理现实中的循环偏好数据，并在LLM集成估值中展示了不同优先级平衡导致的不同估值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优先级感知Shapley值要求成对优先级必须是二值的且无环，这在现实数据（如聚合的人类偏好、多准则比较）中严重违背。需要一种能处理任意有向加权优先级图（允许环）的估值方法。

Method: 定义在任意有向加权优先级图上的随机序值，其中成对边通过惩罚而非禁止顺序违反来工作；通过公理化刻画建立GPASV；开发相关计算方法；引入优先级扫描诊断方法。

Result: 在Chatbot Arena循环偏好图上对LLM集成进行估值，发现优先级感知估值并非一键操作：不同的图优先级与个体软优先级平衡会导致同一数据产生实质不同的估值结果。

Conclusion: GPASV覆盖了多种经典模型作为边界情况，为处理循环偏好数据提供了更灵活的估值框架，但同时也揭示了优先级感知估值的多解性——需要根据具体场景权衡不同优先级来源。

Abstract: Shapley value and its priority-aware extensions are widely used for valuation in machine learning, but existing methods require pairwise priority to be binary and acyclic, a restriction spectacularly violated in real-data examples such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons. We introduce the generalized priority-aware Shapley value (GPASV), a random order value defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs, in which pairwise edges penalize rather than forbid order violations. GPASV covers a range of classical models as boundary cases. We establish GPASV through an axiomatic characterization, develop the associated computational methods, and introduce a priority sweeping diagnostic extending PASV's. We apply GPASV to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, illustrating that priority-aware valuation is not a one-button operation: different balances of pairwise graph priority versus individual soft priority produce substantively different valuations of the same data.

</details>


### [107] [Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15050)
*Yuxin Guo,Dongrui Deng,Pulkit Grover*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验不确定性的结构分解方法，将内在模糊性与推理传播的不确定性分离，并通过级联公式实现校准分析，在MRI和EEG成像中验证了该方法能揭示仅靠重建质量无法发现的模型失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险应用（如医学成像和科学发现）中，后验不确定性难以解释，因为它混合了前向算子的内在模糊性和推理传播的不确定性。现有方法仅通过重建质量选择模型，无法揭示隐藏的失败模式。

Method: 提出后验不确定性的结构分解方法，引入级联（cascade）公式来隔离内在模糊性，使其可用于校准分析，包括定性诊断和基于仿真的校准测试。

Result: 在具有解析后验结构的高斯示例上验证了该方法；在加速MRI上展示了分解效果；在EEG源成像上应用校准诊断，揭示了仅凭重建质量无法发现的失败模式。

Conclusion: 该结构分解方法能够有效分离后验不确定性中的不同来源，为逆问题中的不确定性量化提供了更可靠的校准分析工具，尤其适用于对不确定性有严格要求的高风险应用场景。

Abstract: Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.

</details>


### [108] [An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15051)
*Linghao Kong,Megan Flynn,Michael Peng,Nir Shavit,Mark Kurtz,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单且可解释的延迟模型，用于分析推测解码（Speculative Decoding）在大语言模型生产级推理服务中的行为。通过Little定律推断有效批量大小，并将每请求分解为与负载无关和相关的部分，模型准确描述了延迟变化，解释了加速比在高负载下下降的原因，并扩展到了混合专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码研究多集中在孤立或固定批量设置下，其在真实生产服务系统（负载动态变化、有效批量由系统决定而非直接控制）中的行为尚不明确。

Method: 利用Little定律从请求率推断有效批量大小，将每个请求的处理需求分解为负载无关和负载相关两部分，分别对应预填充、草稿生成和验证阶段，并通过vLLM平台进行广泛测量验证。

Result: 模型准确描述了观察到的延迟；解释了加速比在服务器负载增加时常常下降的原因；刻画了草稿长度、接受率、验证器-草稿器大小对延迟的影响；并扩展至混合专家模型，揭示了稀疏专家激活如何改变不同负载下的有效服务成本。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解推测解码在真实LLM服务系统中的行为提供了一个结构化框架，并为部署系统中的配置优化提供了指导。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a smaller draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model in parallel. While prior work demonstrates substantial speedups in isolated or fixed-batch settings, the behavior of SD in production serving systems remains poorly understood: request load varies over time, and effective batch size emerges from the serving system rather than being directly controlled or observed. In this work, we develop a simple and interpretable latency model for SD in LLM serving. We infer effective batch size from request rate using Little's Law and decompose per-request demand into load-independent and load-dependent components for prefill, drafting, and verification. We validate our model using extensive measurements from vLLM across verifier and drafter model sizes, prefill and decode lengths, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities. The model accurately describes observed latency, explains why speedups often diminish as server load increases, and characterizes how draft length, acceptance rate, and verifier-drafter size shape latency across serving conditions, with implications for configuring SD in deployed systems. We further show how the framework extends to mixture of experts models, where sparse expert activation changes the effective service costs across load regimes. Together, our results provide a structured framework for understanding SD in real LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [109] [TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15053)
*Anurup Ganguli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFGN是一种面向Transformer的架构叠加层，通过输入条件化的参数高效更新和读写分解机制，在无需重放缓冲区、任务标签或正则化惩罚的情况下，解决了大语言模型在异构文本领域上的持续预训练中的灾难性遗忘问题，并在三个模型规模（~398M、~739M、~9B）上实现了接近零的后向迁移损失和优异的跨领域前向迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续预训练方法在异构文本领域（如散文、Python、数学、生物医学、中文、JavaScript）上存在严重局限：重放缓冲区扩展性差、依赖任务标识符、正则化惩罚在大规模下效果不佳，且多数评估仅停留在句子分类尺度。在无重放、无任务标签的LLM规模下，灾难性遗忘问题仍未得到架构层面的解决。

Method: 提出TFGN（Task-Focused Gradient Network），作为Transformer语言模型的架构叠加层，核心思想是读写分解（Read/Write Decomposition）：前向传播保持全密集计算，而跨领域参数更新被结构化为不覆盖先前领域子空间。此外包含两个扩展：(A) 闭环元控制层，模拟System A/System M角色，额外减少81%遗忘；(B) 算子级计划向量，在30个源->目标对上实现99.96%余弦保真度的前向传播重塑。

Result: 在1B token/阶段、六个异构文本领域、三个模型规模（~398M、~739M、~9B）及两种训练模式（From-Scratch和Retrofit）下：(1) LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit上后向迁移为-0.007，HellaSwag保留率为0.506/0.504/0.510；(2) 领域对间梯度分离≥99.59% L2正交；(3) 前向迁移：LLaMA-8B Retrofit上JavaScript PPL下降26.8%，GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch上下降62.0%（纯Python训练产生）。

Conclusion: TFGN是首个在LLM规模上同时解决灾难性遗忘、实现闭环自主学习元控制器、并携带算子级隐式规划器的架构。其读写分解的架构洞见为大规模持续预训练提供了无需重放、无需任务标签的可行路径。

Abstract: Continually pre-training a large language model on heterogeneous text domains, without replay or task labels, has remained an unsolved architectural problem at LLM scale. Existing methods rely on replay buffers, task identifiers, regularization penalties that scale poorly, or sentence-classification-scale evaluation.
  We introduce TFGN, an architectural overlay for transformer language models that produces input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while leaving the rest of the transformer unchanged. On six heterogeneous text domains (Prose, Python, Math, Biomedical, Chinese, JavaScript) at 1B tokens per phase across three model scales (~398M, ~739M, ~9B) and two regimes (From-Scratch and Retrofit), TFGN achieves backward transfer of -0.007 at LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit, HellaSwag retention 0.506/0.504/0.510, and >=99.59% L2-orthogonal gradient separation between domain pairs - with no replay, no task IDs, no Fisher penalty.
  The same matrices show positive cross-domain forward transfer: held-out JavaScript PPL drops 26.8% at LLaMA-8B Retrofit and 62.0% at GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch purely from Python training. Two extensions on the same substrate close further open problems. A closed-loop meta-control layer (Extension A) reduces forgetting by an additional 81% at ~398M, mapping onto the System A and System M roles of Dupoux et al. (arXiv:2603.15381). An operator-level plan vector (Extension B) reshapes forward-pass behavior at 99.96% cosine fidelity over 30 source->target pairs.
  The architectural insight is a Read/Write decomposition: the forward pass is fully dense, while cross-domain parameter updates are structured so prior-domain subspaces are not written to. To our knowledge, TFGN is the first architecture that simultaneously closes catastrophic forgetting at LLM scale, realizes a closed-loop autonomous-learning meta-controller, and carries an operator-level latent planner.

</details>


### [110] [DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15055)
*Quanhao Li,Junqiu Yu,Kaixun Jiang,Yujie Wei,Zhen Xing,Pandeng Li,Ruihang Chu,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffusionOPD，一种基于在线策略蒸馏的多任务扩散模型训练范式，通过先独立训练任务特定教师模型，再将其能力蒸馏到统一学生模型中，有效解决了多任务强化学习中的跨任务干扰和灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在扩散文本到图像模型中仅局限于单任务优化，而扩展到多任务场景时，联合优化面临跨任务干扰和任务不平衡问题，级联RL则笨重且容易灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出DiffusionOPD框架：1) 先为每个任务独立训练任务特定的教师模型；2) 再沿着学生自身的轨迹将教师能力蒸馏到统一学生模型中。理论上将OPD从离散令牌扩展到连续状态马尔可夫过程，推导出闭式每步KL目标，通过均值匹配统一随机SDE和确定性ODE细化，并提供解析梯度替代传统PPO策略梯度。

Result: 实验表明DiffusionOPD在训练效率和最终性能上一致超越多奖励RL和级联RL基线，在所有评估基准上达到SOTA。解析梯度相比PPO策略梯度具有更低的方差和更好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦单任务探索与多任务整合，DiffusionOPD避免了从头联合优化所有任务的负担，为扩散模型的多任务RL训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [Proposal and study of statistical features for string similarity computation and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15110)
*E. O. Rodrigues,D. Casanova,M. Teixeira,V. Pegorini,F. Favarim,E. Clua,A. Conci,Panos Liatsis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将视觉计算中的共生矩阵（COM）和游程长度矩阵（RLM）特征适配到通用字符串（单词、短语、代码、文本）的相似度计算中。这些纯统计特征不依赖语言信息，适用于任何语言和语法结构。在合成实验中，COM和RLM特征优于最长公共子序列、互信息、编辑距离等现有统计方法；在4个案例中有3个的统计显著性显著高于第二优的基于距离的方法（P<0.001）。在真实的文本剽窃数据集上，RLM特征取得了最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字符串相似度计算方法多依赖语言结构或语法信息，缺乏跨语言通用的纯统计特征。视觉计算领域的特征（COM和RLM）在图像分析中表现优异，但其是否适用于通用字符串相似度计算尚未被探索。

Method: 将视觉计算中的共生矩阵（Co-occurrence Matrix）和游程长度矩阵（Run-Length Matrix）适配到字符串分析中，提出基于这些矩阵的统计特征。与最长公共子序列（LCS）、最大连续最长公共子序列、互信息（MI）和编辑距离等基线方法进行对比。设计了合成实验和真实文本剽窃数据集上的实验。

Result: 合成实验中，COM和RLM特征在4个案例中有3个的统计显著性显著高于第二优的基于距离的方法（P<0.001）。在真实文本剽窃数据集上，RLM特征取得了最佳结果。

Conclusion: 提出的COM和RLM统计特征能够有效用于通用字符串相似度计算，且不依赖语言信息，在多个实验场景中优于现有主流统计方法，具有跨语言和跨领域的普适性。

Abstract: Adaptations of features commonly applied in the field of visual computing, co-occurrence matrix (COM) and run-length matrix (RLM), are proposed for the similarity computation of strings in general (words, phrases, codes and texts). The proposed features are not sensitive to language related information. These are purely statistical and can be used in any context with any language or grammatical structure. Other statistical measures that are commonly employed in the field such as longest common subsequence, maximal consecutive longest common subsequence, mutual information and edit distances are evaluated and compared. In the first synthetic set of experiments, the COM and RLM features outperform the remaining state-of-the-art statistical features. In 3 out of 4 cases, the RLM and COM features were statistically more significant than the second best group based on distances (P-value < 0.001). When it comes to a real text plagiarism dataset, the RLM features obtained the best results.

</details>


### [112] [Learning from Language Feedback via Variational Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15113)
*Yang Li,Erik Nijkamp,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Rayhan Joty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出变分策略蒸馏（VPD）框架，通过将语言反馈学习形式化为变分期望最大化问题，使教师策略与学生策略共同进化，克服了被动自蒸馏中教师评估能力天花板问题，在科学推理和代码生成任务上优于标准RLVR和现有自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在复杂推理任务中面临稀疏结果信号导致的探索瓶颈；现有基于语言反馈的在线自蒸馏方法依赖固定的被动教师，当学生策略提升后教师的零样本评估能力会停滞，从而阻碍进一步学习。

Method: 将语言反馈学习形式化为变分期望最大化问题。E步：通过自适应信任域更新主动优化教师策略，将文本反馈转化为动态改进的目标token分布；M步：学生策略在自身的在线策略rollout上内化这种密集分布指导。教师与学生策略共同进化。

Result: 在科学推理和代码生成任务上，使用多种诊断反馈源进行评估，VPD一致优于标准RLVR和现有自蒸馏基线。通过数学推理和冷启动场景的压力测试，揭示了反馈驱动自蒸馏与纯环境驱动RL的根本界限。

Conclusion: VPD通过主动优化教师策略而非被动使用，解决了自蒸馏中教师能力天花板问题，为利用语言反馈进行密集奖励信号提取提供了更有效的框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) suffers from sparse outcome signals, creating severe exploration bottlenecks on complex reasoning tasks. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods attempt to address this by utilizing language feedback to generate dense, token-level supervision. However, these approaches rely on a fixed, passive teacher to interpret the feedback. As the student policy improves, the teacher's zero-shot assessment capabilities plateau, ultimately halting further learning. To overcome this, we propose Variational Policy Distillation (VPD), a framework that formalizes learning from language feedback as a Variational Expectation-Maximization (EM) problem. VPD co-evolves both policies: in the E-step, the teacher is actively refined on trajectory outcomes via an adaptive trust-region update, translating textual feedback into a dynamically improved target token distribution. In the M-step, the student internalizes this dense distributional guidance on its own on-policy rollouts. By continuously improving the teacher's ability to extract actionable signals from textual critique, VPD overcomes the limitations of passive distillation. Evaluated across diverse sources of diagnostic feedback on scientific reasoning and code generation tasks, VPD consistently outperforms both standard RLVR and existing self-distillation baselines. Finally, by stress-testing our framework on rigid mathematical reasoning and cold-start regimes, we illuminate the fundamental bounds of feedback-driven self-distillation compared to pure environment-driven RL.

</details>


### [113] [Natural Synthesis: Outperforming Reactive Synthesis Tools with Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15131)
*Frederik Schmitt,Matthias Cosler,Niklas Metzger,Julian Siber,Vladimir Krsmanovic,Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法，将大型推理模型与模型检查器相结合，通过符号反馈迭代修复综合的Verilog实现，同时引入自然语言自动形式化步骤，使自然语言规格说明的端到端综合工作流成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式综合面临两大挑战：算法上困难，以及手工编写形式化规格说明难度大。本文旨在同时解决这两方面问题。

Method: 采用神经符号方法，耦合大型推理模型与模型检查器，通过声音符号反馈迭代修复综合的Verilog实现；并引入自动形式化步骤，将规格说明任务从时序逻辑迁移到自然语言。

Result: 该方法在年度综合竞赛中解决了比最佳专用工具更多的基准测试，并扩展到了参数化系统的构建；从自然语言出发的性能与从形式化规格说明出发相当。

Conclusion: 自然语言规格说明可以作为反应式综合的可行端到端工作流，神经符号方法在算法效率和易用性上均有显著提升。

Abstract: Reactive synthesis, the problem of automatically constructing a hardware circuit from a logical specification, is a long-standing challenge in formal verification. It is elusive for two reasons: It is algorithmically hard, and writing formal specifications by hand is notoriously difficult. In this paper, we tackle both sides of the problem. For the algorithmic side, we present a neuro-symbolic approach to reactive synthesis that couples large reasoning models with model checkers to iteratively repair a synthesized Verilog implementation via sound symbolic feedback. Our approach solves more benchmarks than the best dedicated tools in the annual synthesis competition and extends to constructing parameterized systems, a problem known to be undecidable. On the specification side, we introduce an autoformalization step that shifts the specification task from temporal logic to natural language by introducing a hand-authored dataset of natural-language specifications for evaluation. We demonstrate performance comparable to that of starting from formal specifications, establishing natural synthesis as a viable end-to-end workflow.

</details>


### [114] [Causal Foundation Models with Continuous Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15133)
*Christopher Stith,Medha Barath,Vahid Balazadeh,Jesse C. Cresswell,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向连续治疗设置的因果基础模型，通过元学习在无需额外训练的情况下预测未见任务中的因果效应，利用Transformer和上下文学习来摊销贝叶斯后验推断，在个体治疗反应曲线重建任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续治疗设置（干预变量为连续值）在多个领域具有重要意义，但相比二元治疗设置研究较少，现有模型需要表示跨连续治疗值的效应，缺乏能够迁移学习的因果基础模型。

Method: 设计了一个面向连续治疗变量的数据生成过程先验，生成丰富的因果训练语料；训练Transformer模型仅根据观测数据重建个体治疗-反应曲线，利用上下文学习来摊销昂贵的贝叶斯后验推断。

Result: 在个体治疗反应曲线重建任务上，该模型超越了专门针对这些任务训练的因果模型，达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 连续治疗设置的因果基础模型是可行的，通过元学习和上下文学习能够有效泛化到未见任务，为因果推断领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Causal inference, estimating causal effects from observational data, is a fundamental tool in many disciplines. Of particular importance across a variety of domains is the continuous treatment setting, where the variable of intervention has a continuous range. This setting is far less explored and represents a substantial shift from the binary treatment setting, with models needing to represent effects across a continuum of treatment values. In this paper, we present the first causal foundation model for the continuous treatment setting. Our model meta-learns the ability to predict causal effects across a wide variety of unseen tasks without additional training or fine-tuning. First, we design a novel prior over data-generating processes with continuous treatment variables in order to generate a rich causal training corpus. We then train a transformer to reconstruct individual treatment-response curves given only observational data, leveraging in-context learning to amortize expensive Bayesian posterior inference. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on individual treatment-response curve reconstruction tasks compared to causal models which are trained specifically for those tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Training ML Models with Predictable Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15134)
*Will Schwarzer,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的偏差分析方法，用于预测机器学习模型在部署规模下的故障率，并设计了一种新的微调目标（预测性损失）来减少预测误差，在密码游戏和网格世界实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署前安全评估中，评估集通常太小，无法观察到部署时真正关键的高频故障。现有外推方法（Jones et al., 2025）虽可预测部署规模故障率，但其预测误差的来源和偏差方向尚未被充分理解，特别是当评估集遗漏了部署集中存在的罕见高故障模式时，会导致低估风险。

Method: 1) 对Jones et al. (2025)的基于top-k故障分数的外推估计器进行有限k分解，揭示其固有偏差（偏向过度预测，即安全有利方向）；2) 识别当评估集遗漏部署集的罕见高故障模式时，估计器会产生欠预测（安全不利方向）；3) 提出预测性损失（forecastability loss）作为微调目标，使模型更易于预测其故障分布；4) 在语言模型密码游戏和RL网格世界两个概念验证实验中进行验证。

Result: 在两个实验（语言模型密码游戏和RL网格世界）中，使用预测性损失进行微调显著降低了留出集上的预测误差，同时保持了主要任务能力，并实现了与监督基线相当的安全性。

Conclusion: 预测性损失能有效缓解因评估集遗漏罕见高故障模式而导致的部署规模故障率低估问题，为预部署安全评估提供了更可靠的预测工具，且不会牺牲模型主要任务性能。

Abstract: Estimating how often an ML model will fail at deployment scale is central to pre-deployment safety assessment, but a feasible evaluation set is rarely large enough to observe the failures that matter. Jones et al. (2025) address this by extrapolating from the largest k failure scores in an evaluation set to predict deployment-scale failure rates. We give a finite-k decomposition of this estimator's forecast error and show that it has a built-in bias toward over-prediction in the typical case, which is the safety-favorable direction. This bias is offset when the evaluation set misses a rare high-failure mode that the deployment set contains, leaving the forecast to under-predict at deployment scale. We propose a fine-tuning objective, the forecastability loss, that addresses this failure mode. In two proof-of-concept experiments, a language-model password game and an RL gridworld, fine-tuning substantially reduces held-out forecast error while preserving primary-task capability and achieving safety similar to that of supervised baselines.

</details>


### [116] [Forgetting That Sticks: Quantization-Permanent Unlearning via Circuit Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15138)
*Saisab Sadhu,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示量化恢复机器遗忘是系统性问题，根因在于参数更新幅度远低于量化桶宽（47-828倍），提出MANSU方法通过因果电路归因、零空间投影和对角Fisher约束实现量化鲁棒的遗忘，并引入CAD机制验证指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准遗忘评估在训练后立即进行全精度测试，但实际部署的语言模型都经过量化。近期工作发现4-bit后训练量化可以逆转机器遗忘，本文旨在探究这是否是系统性失败及其根本原因。

Method: 提出MANSU方法，包含三个核心组件：1）因果电路归因（识别最小遗忘子图）；2）电路受限零空间投影（结合对角Fisher保留约束）；3）逐参数幅度下限（保证量化生存）。同时提出CAD机制验证指标。

Result: MANSU是首个同时满足遗忘有效性、保留保持性、非正PTQ差距和结构性擦除四个属性的方法，而梯度基线方法在压缩下恢复高达+0.05的准确率。

Conclusion: 量化恢复遗忘是系统性双重失败：梯度方法在压缩下丢失遗忘效果，而能抵抗量化的方法几乎不改变模型。MANSU通过确保参数更新跨越量化桶边界来解决此问题。

Abstract: Standard unlearning evaluations measure behavioral suppression in full precision, immediately after training, despite every deployed language model being quantized first. Recent work has shown that 4-bit post-training quantization can reverse machine unlearning; we show this is not a tuning artefact but a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods that achieve meaningful forgetting lose it under compression, while methods that survive quantization barely change the model. Both failures trace to the same root cause: across all baselines, per-parameter updates lie 47-828x below the NF4 quantization bin width; updates diffused across billions of parameters cannot clear quantization bin boundaries, a consequence we formalize as a sparsity-permanence tradeoff. We present MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning), which resolves both modes by combining causal circuit attribution to isolate the minimal forget-set subgraph, circuit-restricted null-space projection with a diagonal-Fisher retain bound, and a per-parameter magnitude floor guaranteeing quantization survival by construction. We additionally introduce Circuit Attribution Divergence (CAD), a mechanistic verification metric distinguishing structural erasure from behavioral suppression, a distinction existing metrics cannot make. Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.

</details>


### [117] [Widening the Gap: Exploiting LLM Quantization via Outlier Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15152)
*Xiaohua Zhan,Kazuki Egashira,Robin Staab,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对先进量化方法（AWQ、GPTQ、GGUF I-quants等）的量化条件攻击，利用量化中异常值导致其他权重归零的特性，通过注入异常值诱导权重坍缩，使全精度模型看似正常但在量化后表现出恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化条件攻击仅适用于简单量化方法，无法攻破AWQ、GPTQ等先进量化方案，限制了攻击的实际影响。亟需一种能针对广泛使用的复杂量化方法的攻击方案。

Method: 利用现代量化方法共有的特性：大异常值会导致其他权重被四舍五入为零。攻击者通过在特定权重块中注入异常值，诱导目标性的、可预测的权重坍缩，从而构造看似良性但在量化后展现恶意行为的全精度模型。

Result: 在多种攻击场景和多个LLM上的广泛评估表明，该方法对先前攻击失败的多种量化方法取得了高成功率。

Conclusion: 首次证明量化安全风险不仅限于简单量化方案，而是广泛存在于复杂、常用的量化方法中。

Abstract: LLM quantization has become essential for memory-efficient deployment. Recent work has shown that quantization schemes can pose critical security risks: an adversary may release a model that appears benign in full precision but exhibits malicious behavior once quantized by users. However, existing quantization-conditioned attacks have been limited to relatively simple quantization methods, where the attacker can estimate weight regions that remain invariant under the target quantization. Notably, prior attacks have consistently failed to compromise more popular and sophisticated schemes, limiting their practical impact. In this work, we introduce the first quantization-conditioned attack that consistently induces malicious behavior that can be triggered by a broad range of advanced quantization techniques, including AWQ, GPTQ, and GGUF I-quants. Our attack exploits a simple property shared by many modern quantization methods: large outliers can cause other weights to be rounded to zero. Consequently, by injecting outliers into specific weight blocks, an adversary can therefore induce a targeted, predictable weight collapse in the model. This effect can be used to craft seemingly benign full-precision models that exhibit a wide range of malicious behaviors after quantization. Through extensive evaluation across three attack scenarios and LLMs, we show that our attack achieves high success rates against a broad range of quantization methods on which prior attacks fail. Our results demonstrate, for the first time, that the security risks of quantization are not restricted to simpler schemes but are broadly relevant across complex, widely-used quantization methods.

</details>


### [118] [Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15155)
*Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Jinyang Wu,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SDAR（自蒸馏智能体强化学习）将OPSD作为门控辅助目标，通过sigmoid门控机制选择性地利用教师分支的稠密token级监督信号，解决多轮交互中OPSD的不稳定性问题，在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上相较GRPO平均提升约8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹级奖励信号在长程交互中仅提供粗粒度监督，而OPSD在多轮场景下存在累积不稳定性和教师拒绝信号不对称处理问题。

Method: SDAR将OPSD作为门控辅助目标，RL为主优化骨干；将解耦的token级信号映射到sigmoid门控，强化教师认可的正差距token蒸馏，软衰减教师拒绝信号。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Qwen3系列上，SDAR相较GRPO提升：ALFWorld +9.4%，Search-QA +7.0%，WebShop-Acc +10.2%；避免GRPO+OPSD的不稳定性，一致超越混合RL-OPSD基线。

Conclusion: SDAR有效地将稠密token级指导引入多轮智能体强化学习，解决了OPSD的稳定性与不对称性问题，显著提升性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a central paradigm for post-training LLM agents, yet its trajectory-level reward signal provides only coarse supervision for long-horizon interaction. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) complements RL by introducing dense token-level guidance from a teacher branch augmented with privileged context. However, transferring OPSD to multi-turn agents proves problematic: compounding multi-turn instability destabilizes supervision, while skill-conditioned privileged guidance requires asymmetric treatment for negative teacher rejections may arise from imperfect skills retrieval or utilization. We introduce SDAR (Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning), which treats OPSD as a gated auxiliary objective while keeping RL as the primary optimization backbone. SDAR maps detached token-level signals into a sigmoid gate, strengthening distillation on teacher-endorsed positive-gap tokens and softly attenuating negative teacher rejections. Across the Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 families on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA, SDAR substantially improves over GRPO (+9.4% on ALFWorld, +7.0% on Search-QA, +10.2% on WebShop-Acc), avoids the instability of naive GRPO+OPSD, and consistently outperforms hybrid RL--OPSD baselines across model scales.

</details>


### [119] [When Are Two Networks the Same? Tensor Similarity for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15183)
*ML Nissen Gonzalez,Melwina Albuquerque,Laurence Wroe,Jacob Meyer Cohen,Logan Riggs Smith,Thomas Dooms*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于权重的张量相似度度量，该度量对权重空间对称性具有不变性，能够捕捉全局功能等价性并利用高效递归算法处理跨层机制，在追踪功能训练动态（如grokking和后门插入）方面优于现有指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相似度度量要么基于经验行为（对分布外机制不敏感），要么基于依赖基的参数（忽略权重空间对称性），无法可靠验证两个模型部分是否实现相同计算。

Method: 针对张量基模型类别，提出一种对权重空间对称性不变的张量相似度度量；通过高效递归算法实现跨层机制的全局功能等价性捕捉。

Result: 在追踪功能训练动态（如grokking和后门插入）方面，张量相似度比现有指标具有更高的保真度。

Conclusion: 将测量相似性和验证忠实性从一个经验近似问题转化为一个可解的代数问题。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to break models into meaningful parts; verifying that two such parts implement the same computation is a prerequisite. Existing similarity measures evaluate either empirical behaviour, leaving them blind to out-of-distribution mechanisms, or basis-dependent parameters, meaning they disregard weight-space symmetries. To address these issues for the class of tensor-based models, we introduce a weight-based metric, tensor similarity, that is invariant to such symmetries. This metric captures global functional equivalence and accounts for cross-layer mechanisms using an efficient recursive algorithm. Empirically, tensor similarity tracks functional training dynamics, such as grokking and backdoor insertion, with higher fidelity than existing metrics. This reduces measuring similarity and verifying faithfulness into a solved algebraic problem rather than one of empirical approximation.

</details>


### [120] [FutureSim: Replaying World Events to Evaluate Adaptive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15188)
*Shashwat Goel,Nikhil Chandak,Arvindh Arun,Ameya Prabhu,Steffen Staab,Moritz Hardt,Maksym Andriushchenko,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FutureSim 基准，通过回放真实世界新闻事件来评估 AI 智能体在动态开放环境中的适应能力。在 2026 年 1-3 月的预测任务中，最佳智能体准确率仅 25%，许多智能体的 Brier 技能分数甚至不如不做预测。该基准为研究长时序测试时自适应、搜索、记忆和不确定性推理等方向提供了现实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对 AI 智能体在动态、开放环境中适应新信息能力的真实评估，需要构建基于真实世界事件回放的模拟环境来测量这一关键能力。

Method: 构建 FutureSim 仿真环境，按时间顺序回放真实新闻文章和问题解析过程，要求智能体在知识截止日期后预测世界事件（2026 年 1-3 月），同时与不断到来的新闻流进行交互。

Result: 最佳智能体准确率仅 25%，许多智能体的 Brier 技能分数低于不做任何预测的基线。通过消融实验揭示了 FutureSim 在研究长时序测试时自适应、搜索、记忆和不确定性推理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FutureSim 为衡量 AI 在真实世界长时序开放环境中的适应能力提供了可行的基准设计，有助于推动相关研究方向的发展。

Abstract: AI agents are being increasingly deployed in dynamic, open-ended environments that require adapting to new information as it arrives. To efficiently measure this capability for realistic use-cases, we propose building grounded simulations that replay real-world events in the order they occurred. We build FutureSim, where agents forecast world events beyond their knowledge cutoff while interacting with a chronological replay of the world: real news articles arriving and questions resolving over the simulated period. We evaluate frontier agents in their native harness, testing their ability to predict world events over a three-month period from January to March 2026. FutureSim reveals a clear separation in their capabilities, with the best agent's accuracy being 25%, and many having worse Brier skill score than making no prediction at all. Through careful ablations, we show how FutureSim offers a realistic setting to study emerging research directions like long-horizon test-time adaptation, search, memory, and reasoning about uncertainty. Overall, we hope our benchmark design paves the way to measure AI progress on open-ended adaptation spanning long time-horizons in the real world.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Towards Robotic Dexterous Hand Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13925)
*Weiguang Zhao,Xihao Guo,Tian Liang,Rui Zhang,Irwin King,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对灵巧手研究进行了全面综述，从硬件设计、控制与学习方法、数据集与评估协议四个互补维度系统梳理了该领域的关键权衡、方法论演进轨迹及未来开放挑战，旨在建立结构化理解并阐明发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手研究在手部形态、传感配置、任务设定、训练数据和评估协议等方面存在显著异质性，导致系统比较困难，难以清晰把握领域发展脉络。本文旨在通过多维度综合分析弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 从四个互补方面开展综述：(1)硬件层面分析驱动、传动、感知及代表性手型设计的权衡；(2)方法论层面按主要范式分组，按时间顺序梳理控制与学习方法演进；(3)整合数据集、模态设计与评估实践；(4)讨论当前局限并总结未来方向。

Result: 揭示了灵巧手研究中力能力、柔顺性、带宽、集成度与系统复杂度之间的关键权衡，梳理了控制与学习方法从传统范式到深度学习的演进脉络，并指出了数据集与评估协议标准化不足等核心问题。

Conclusion: 通过连接硬件分析、方法论发展、数据资源与评估体系，本文为灵巧手研究提供了结构化理解，并明确了未来最关键的研究挑战，有助于推动该领域走向更系统化的发展。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous hands are central to contact-rich manipulation, with rapid progress driven by advances in hardware, sensing, control, simulation, and data generation. However, existing studies are often developed under different assumptions regarding hand embodiments, sensory configurations, task settings, training data, and evaluation protocols, making systematic comparison difficult and obscuring the developmental trajectory of the field. This survey provides a holistic review of dexterous hand research from four complementary aspects. First, we present a hardware-level analysis covering actuation, transmission, perception, and representative hand designs, highlighting the key trade-offs in force capability, compliance, bandwidth, integration, and system complexity. Furthermore, we review control and learning methods for dexterous manipulation from a methodological perspective, grouping representative works by major paradigms and tracing their evolution in chronological order. In addition, we consolidate datasets, modality design, and evaluation practices, which enables methodological progress to be interpreted together with the ways in which it is trained, benchmarked, and assessed. Finally, we discuss the major limitations of current dexterous hand research and summarize the corresponding future directions. By connecting hardware analysis, methodological development, data resources, and evaluation, this survey aims to provide a structured understanding of dexterous hand research and to clarify the most important open challenges for future study.

</details>


### [122] [Ergodic Imitation for Adaptive Exploration around Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13996)
*Ziyi Xu,Cem Bilaloglu,Yiming Li,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应遍历模仿学习方法，通过从演示构建目标分布，在跟踪与探索之间自适应插值，解决模仿学习中训练与部署条件不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在模仿学习中面临训练与部署条件不匹配（环境变化、观测与控制不完善），导致机器人沿名义轨迹执行时可能卡住并失败，需要自适应的在线探索策略。

Method: 提出自适应遍历模仿方法，从检索到的演示几何中构建目标分布，利用该分布生成轨迹，在跟踪与探索之间自适应插值；将遍历控制扩展到模仿学习，融入基于检索的滚动时域框架。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 该方法将遍历控制从传统的区域覆盖和搜索扩展到自适应模仿学习，通过检索式滚动时域框架实现演示引导下的在线自适应探索。

Abstract: In robotics, a common challenge in imitation learning is the mismatch between training and deployment conditions, caused, for example, by environmental changes or imperfect observation and control. When a robot follows a nominal trajectory under such mismatch, it may become stuck and fail to complete the task. This calls for adaptive online exploration strategies that remain grounded in demonstrations. To this end, we propose an adaptive ergodic imitation approach that constructs a target distribution from the geometry of the retrieved demonstrations and uses it to generate trajectories that adaptively interpolate between tracking and exploration. Our method extends ergodic control beyond its traditional role in area-coverage and search by incorporating demonstrations into a retrieval-based receding-horizon framework for adaptive imitation.

</details>


### [123] [Behavior Cloning for Active Perception with Low-Resolution Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14106)
*Anthony Bilic,Chen Chen,Ladislau Bölöni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究行为克隆是否足以在结构化物体寻找任务中产生主动感知。使用低成本机器人臂和腕部RGB相机，通过低分辨率图像预测关节命令，实现植物重新定位和抓取。结果表明，低分辨率视觉足以完成任务，预测相对关节增量优于绝对位置预测，行为克隆可产生视觉驱动的主动感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究行为克隆（模仿学习）是否足以让机器人产生主动感知能力，即通过主动调整视角来获取更好的观察信息以完成任务，而无需复杂的强化学习或任务规划。

Method: 使用低成本机器人臂和腕部安装的以自我为中心的RGB相机，在结构化物体寻找任务中，通过行为克隆从低分辨率RGB图像直接预测关节命令，实现闭环控制下的主动感知。对比了预测相对关节增量与绝对关节位置两种策略。

Result: 低分辨率以自我为中心的视觉足以可靠完成任务；预测相对关节增量显著优于预测绝对关节位置。

Conclusion: 视觉驱动的主动感知可以通过行为克隆在可复现的环境中实现，为低成本机器人利用模仿学习获得主动感知能力提供了证据。

Abstract: We investigate whether behavior cloning is sufficient to produce active perception in a structured object-finding task. A low-cost robot arm equipped with a wrist-mounted egocentric RGB camera must reposition to center a partially visible plant before triggering a grasp signal, requiring actions that improve future observations. The model predicts joint commands directly from low-resolution RGB images under closed-loop control. We show that low-resolution egocentric vision is sufficient for reliable task completion and that predicting relative joint deltas substantially outperforms absolute joint position prediction in our setting. These results demonstrate that visually grounded active perception can emerge from behavior cloning in a reproducible setting.

</details>


### [124] [Motion Planning for Autonomous Vehicles using Optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14199)
*Matheus Wagner,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究利用凸集图（GCS）优化来近似自动驾驶中的非线性最优控制问题，通过凸区域图表示自由空间、贝塞尔曲线参数化路径以及多项式时间缩放函数，在保持凸性的同时生成无碰撞且动力学可行的轨迹，实验表明该方法在计算效率和初始化鲁棒性上优于传统非线性规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性最优控制方法计算量大且对初始化敏感，而几何规划方法虽可扩展但常将路径选择与轨迹优化解耦，缺乏高效的结构化近似方法。

Method: 将自由空间表示为有向凸区域图，采用贝塞尔曲线参数化空间路径、多项式时间缩放函数处理时间演化，基于小滑移和线性轮胎假设的简化动力学自行车模型，通过凸约束近似保证动力学可行性。

Result: 在CommonRoad场景测试中，GCS方法能生成与非线性规划高度匹配的无碰撞且动力学一致的轨迹，同时计算效率更高，对初始化的敏感度更低。

Conclusion: GCS提供了一种结构化的非线性运动规划近似方法，在连续松弛中保持凸性的同时，有效捕捉了主要的几何和动力学效应。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires generating collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in complex environments under real-time constraints. While nonlinear optimal control formulations provide high-fidelity solutions, they are computationally demanding and sensitive to initialization, whereas geometric planning methods scale well but often decouple path selection from trajectory optimization. This paper studies the extent to which optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) can approximate solutions of nonlinear optimal control problems in the context of autonomous driving. The free space is represented as a finite union of convex regions organized as a directed graph, allowing nonconvex geometry to be handled through discrete connectivity decisions while maintaining convex trajectory constraints within each region. Vehicle motion is parameterized using Bezier curves for the spatial path and a polynomial time-scaling function for temporal evolution. Under small-slip and linear tire assumptions, a simplified dynamic bicycle model enables approximate enforcement of dynamic feasibility through convex constraints on trajectory derivatives. The approach is evaluated in CommonRoad scenarios involving static obstacle avoidance and lane-changing maneuvers, and is compared against a nonlinear discrete-time optimal control formulation. The results indicate that the GCS-based method generates collision-free and dynamically consistent trajectories that closely match those obtained from the nonlinear program, while exhibiting improved computational efficiency and reduced sensitivity to initialization. These findings suggest that GCS provides a structured approximation of nonlinear motion planning problems, capturing dominant geometric and dynamic effects while preserving convexity in the continuous relaxation.

</details>


### [125] [MAPLE: Latent Multi-Agent Play for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14201)
*Rajeev Yasarla,Deepti Hegde,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Shizhong Han,Yunxiao Shi,Meysam Sadeghigooghari,Hanno Ackermann,Litian Liu,Pranav Desai,Fatih Porikli,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPLE是一个针对VLA模型的闭环多智能体训练框架，通过在潜在空间中进行反应式多步 rollout，结合监督微调和强化学习（含多样性奖励），在不依赖外部模拟器的情况下实现了Bench2Drive上的最优驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在闭环评估中表现脆弱，原因在于它们在传统模仿学习框架下训练，而现有的闭环监督方法缺乏可扩展性且无法完全建模反应式环境。

Method: MAPLE包含两个训练阶段：(1) 基于真实轨迹的潜在rollout监督微调；(2) 使用全局和智能体特定奖励的强化学习，并引入多样性奖励以鼓励生成日志数据中不存在的规划行为。该方法在VLA模型的潜在空间中独立控制主车和附近交通智能体进行多步反应式rollout。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上取得了最先进的驾驶性能，证明了可扩展的闭环多智能体交互对鲁棒端到端自动驾驶系统的有效性。

Conclusion: MAPLE提供了一种可扩展的闭环训练框架，无需外部模拟器，通过潜在空间中的反应式多智能体rollout显著提升了VLA模型的闭环驾驶鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective as end-to-end motion planners, but can be brittle when evaluated in closed-loop settings due to being trained under traditional imitation learning framework. Existing closed-loop supervision approaches lack scalability and fail to completely model a reactive environment. We propose MAPLE, a novel framework for reactive, multi-agent rollout of a dynamic driving scenario in the latent space of the VLA model. The ego vehicle and nearby traffic agents are independently controlled over multi-step horizons, while being reactive to other agents in the scene, enabling closed-loop training. MAPLE consists of two training stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on the latent rollouts based on ground-truth trajectories, followed by (2) reinforcement learning with global and agent -specific rewards that encourage safety, progress, and interaction realism. We further propose diversity rewards that encourage the model to generate planning behaviors that may not be present in logged driving data. Notably, our closed-loop training framework is scalable and does not require external simulators, which can be computationally expensive to run and have limited visual fidelity to the real-world. MAPLE achieves state-of-the-art driving performance on Bench2Drive and demonstrates scalable, closed-loop multi-agent play for robust E2E autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [126] [Reactive Planning based Control for Mobile Robots in Obstacle-Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14232)
*Li Tan,Junlin Xiong,Yan Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对部分环境信息下的移动机器人避障运动控制问题，提出一种基于反应式规划的控制策略（RPCS），通过连接初始位置与目标位置生成参考轨迹，利用反应式规划策略（RPS）基于局部信息局部修正轨迹以避障，并结合自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS）通过离散化技术跟踪修正后的轨迹，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在障碍物密集环境中仅有部分环境信息，需从初始位置无碰撞地运动到目标位置，现有方法在信息不完整条件下的有效性和实时性存在挑战。

Method: 首先连接初始与目标位置生成参考轨迹；然后开发反应式规划策略（RPS）基于局部环境信息局部修正参考轨迹以实现避障；再提出自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS）利用离散化技术跟踪可能含局部修正的参考轨迹；最后将RPS与ATCS结合形成RPCS。

Result: 通过数值算例验证了所提RPCS策略的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: RPCS能够在部分环境信息条件下有效实现移动机器人的无碰撞运动控制。

Abstract: This paper addresses the motion control problem for mobile robots in obstacle-cluttered environments. The mobile robot has partial environment information only, and aims to move from an initial position to a target position without collisions. For this purpose, a reactive planning based control strategy (RPCS) is proposed. First, the initial and target positions are connected as a reference trajectory. Then, a reactive planning strategy (RPS) is developed to ensure the collision avoidance by modifying the reference trajectory locally based on the partial environment information. Next, an adaptive tracking control strategy (ATCS) is proposed to track the reference trajectory with potentially local modifications via the discretization techniques. Finally, the RPS and ATCS are combined to establish the RPCS, whose efficacy and advantages are illustrated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [127] [Distill: Uncovering the True Intent behind Human-Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14262)
*Ting Li,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自然语言和终端用户编程在表达用户意图时的不足（前者模糊，后者过于具体），提出Distill方法，通过移除冗余步骤、泛化步骤含义、放宽顺序约束来提炼用户真实意图，并在网页界面和众包实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言模糊不精确、终端用户编程过于具体，现有的人机通信接口难以完整捕捉用户真实意图，这成为人机交互的核心挑战。

Method: 提出Distill方法，包含三个核心操作：(1) 移除不必要的步骤；(2) 泛化单个步骤背后的含义；(3) 放宽步骤之间的顺序约束。在网页界面上实现，并通过众包研究进行验证。

Result: 众包实验表明Distill能够从初始任务规约中有效地引出并精炼用户意图。

Conclusion: Distill提供了一种新的意图提炼机制，弥补了自然语言与终端用户编程之间的鸿沟，有助于提升人机通信系统中用户意图捕捉的准确性。

Abstract: As robots become increasingly integrated into everyday environments, intuitive communication paradigms such as natural language and end-user programming have become indispensable for specifying autonomous robot behavior. However, these mechanisms are ineffective at fully capturing user intent: natural language is imprecise and ambiguous, whereas end-user programming can be overly specific. As a result, understanding what users truly mean when they interact with robots remains a central challenge for human-AI communication systems. To address this issue, we propose the Distill approach for human-robot communication interfaces. Given a task specification provided by the user, Distill (1) removes unnecessary steps; (2) generalizes the meaning behind individual steps; and (3) relaxes ordering constraints between steps. We implemented Distill on a web interface and, through a crowdsourcing study, demonstrated its ability to elicit and refine user intent from initial task specifications.

</details>


### [128] [SeaVis: Modeling and Control of a Remotely Operated Towed Vehicle for Seabed Visualization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14683)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Aske Alstrup,Frederik Rasmussen,Yury Brodskiy,Andriy Sarabakha,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对高分辨率海床测绘中的水下机器人定位问题，提出了一种SeaVis型遥控拖曳体(ROTV)的数学模型，并开发了增益调度线性二次型调节器(LQR)用于深度与姿态控制。高保真仿真表明，相比传统PID控制器，LQR在鲁棒性、控制效率和 flap 驱动方面均表现更优，且增益调度在全程运行速度范围内有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率海床测绘需要水下机器人具备稳定且精确的定位能力，现有控制方法难以满足复杂海床环境下的鲁棒性和效率需求。

Method: 提出SeaVis ROTV的新型数学模型，设计增益调度线性二次型调节器(LQR)进行深度与姿态控制，并在高保真仿真环境中与PID控制器进行对比验证。

Result: LQR相比传统PID控制器具有更优的抗扰动鲁棒性、更高的控制效率，且大幅降低了 flap 驱动幅度；增益调度确保了全运行速度范围内的控制有效性。

Conclusion: 该增益调度LQR方法有效提升了ROTV在复杂海床环境下的控制性能，且仿真环境和控制器均已开源。

Abstract: High-resolution seafloor mapping necessitates stable and precise positioning for underwater robots. This paper introduces a novel mathematical model for SeaVis remotely operated towed vehicles (ROTVs) and develops a gain-scheduled linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for robust depth and attitude control. We validate the approach in a high-fidelity simulation, benchmarking the LQR against a conventional PID controller over a challenging seabed profile. The presented results demonstrate the LQR's superior performance, with significantly enhanced robustness to disturbances, greater control efficiency, and substantially reduced flap actuation. The gain scheduling also confirms the controller's effectiveness across the full operational velocity range. The complete simulation environment and controller are open-sourced.

</details>


### [129] [Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14411)
*Pramod Pal,Shishir Kolathaya,Ashitava Ghosal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究四足机器人足部柔顺性对行走效率的影响，通过在强化学习控制器中集成不同刚度的弹簧足部，发现中等刚度的弹簧相比过刚或过柔的足部可降低约17%的能耗，表明合适的足部柔顺性能在不牺牲稳定性的前提下提升运动效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四足机器人常采用刚性足部以简化控制和维持稳定接触，但这种方法限制了腿部吸收冲击力和储存弹性势能的能力，导致运动能耗较高。本文旨在探究足部柔顺性是否能为四足机器人运动效率带来优势。

Method: 在强化学习(RL)运动控制器中集成足部柔顺性，在仿真中训练了对应8种不同弹簧刚度值的策略，并交叉评估每米行进消耗的机械能；同时在开发的四足机器人平台上进行实物实验验证。

Result: 实物实验中，中等刚度弹簧的能耗比过刚或过柔的弹簧足部降低了约17%，仿真结果呈现相似趋势。

Conclusion: 选择合适的足部柔顺性可以在不破坏机器人运动稳定性的前提下，显著提升四足机器人的运动效率。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are often designed with rigid feet to simplify control and maintain stable contact during locomotion. While this approach is straightforward, it limits the ability of the legs to absorb impact forces and reuse stored elastic energy, leading to higher energy expenditure during locomotion. To explore whether compliant feet can provide an advantage, we integrate foot compliance into a reinforcement learning (RL) locomotion controller and study its effect on walking efficiency. In simulation, we train eight policies corresponding to eight different spring stiffness values and then cross-evaluate their performance by measuring mechanical energy consumed per meter traveled. In experiments done on a developed quadruped, the energy consumption for the intermediate stiffness spring is lower by ~ 17% when compared to a very stiff or a very flexible spring incorporated in the feet, with similar trends appearing in the simulation results. These results indicate that selecting an appropriate foot compliance can improve locomotion efficiency without destabilizing the robot during motion.

</details>


### [130] [Behavioral Data-Driven Optimal Trajectory Generation for Rotary Cranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14944)
*Iskandar Khemakhem,Manuel Zobel,Johannes Schüle,Oliver Sawodny,Naoki Uchiyama,Abdallah Farrage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为数据的旋转起重机开环回转轨迹生成框架，利用Willems基本引理直接从输入-输出数据中学习系统行为，无需显式建模，通过凸优化生成平滑最优轨迹。在实验室起重机平台上验证，相比传统模型方法，负载摆动减少35%，跟踪误差减少43%，运行时间减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑行业增长与全球熟练劳动力短缺背景下，起重机控制自动化对安全高效作业至关重要。传统基于模型的控制方法依赖精确动力学模型和专家调参，难以满足安全和精度要求；而许多学习方法需要大量数据和计算资源。因此需要一种数据驱动且高效的轨迹生成方法。

Method: 基于Willems基本引理及其推广的行为数据驱动框架，绕过显式系统建模，直接利用测量到的输入-输出数据构建系统行为的非参数表示。通过凸优化生成平滑最优轨迹，减少负载摆动、运行时间和能量消耗，降低对专家知识的依赖。

Result: 在实验室起重机平台上验证，与已建立的基于模型的方法相比：负载摆动减少35%，跟踪误差减少43%，运行时间减少50%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动方法能够有效利用有限数据和凸优化生成优质轨迹，在欠驱动起重机动力学条件下表现出色，显著优于传统模型方法。

Abstract: With the growth of the construction industry and the global shortage of skilled labor, the automation of crane control has become increasingly important for safe and efficient operations. A central challenge in automatic crane control is the reduction of load oscillations during motion, which is primarily addressed through appropriate slewing trajectories. In this context, classical model-based control methods rely on accurate dynamical models and expert tuning, and often struggle to meet safety and precision requirements, while many learning-based approaches require large data sets and significant computational resources. This paper proposes a behavioral data-driven framework for generating open-loop slewing trajectories for rotary cranes that suppress load sway while reducing operation time and energy consumption. The approach builds on Willems' fundamental lemma and its generalizations, to bypass explicit system modeling and operate directly on measured input-output data. A practical workflow is presented in this paper to reduce the need for expert knowledge. Despite the underactuated nature of the crane dynamics, the method identifies a nonparametric representation of the system behavior and generates smooth, optimal trajectories using limited data and convex optimization. The proposed trajectory generation method is validated on a laboratory crane setup and compared against an established model-based approach, achieving up to 35% reduction in load sway, 43% reduction in tracking error, and 50% reduction in travel time.

</details>


### [131] [Before the Body Moves: Learning Anticipatory Joint Intent for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14417)
*Haozhe Jia,Honglei Jin,Yuan Zhang,Youcheng Fan,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Shuxu Jin,Kuimou Yu,Zinuo Zhang,Jianfei Song,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAJI是一个层次化框架，通过动力学对齐的联合意图接口连接语言生成与闭环控制，在流式全身控制中实现94.42%的滚动成功率和0.152的FID分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件人形机器人系统通常生成运动学参考（需低级跟踪器被动修复）或使用隐式策略（无法显式编码即将到来的接触变化、支撑转移和平衡准备），缺乏可执行且具有前瞻性的控制表示。

Method: 提出DAJI框架，包含两个核心模块：DAJI-Act通过学生驱动滚动将未来感知教师蒸馏为可部署的扩散动作策略；DAJI-Flow从语言和意图历史中自回归生成未来意图块。

Result: 在HumanML3D风格生成任务上达到94.42%滚动成功率，在BABEL上实现0.152子序列FID。

Conclusion: DAJI在前瞻性隐式学习、单指令生成和流式指令跟随方面均取得优异表现，验证了动力学对齐的联合意图接口在语言条件人形机器人全身控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Natural language is an intuitive interface for humanoid robots, yet streaming whole-body control requires control representations that are executable now and anticipatory of future physical transitions. Existing language-conditioned humanoid systems typically generate kinematic references that a low-level tracker must repair reactively, or use latent/action policies whose outputs do not explicitly encode upcoming contact changes, support transfers, and balance preparation. We propose \textbf{DAJI} (\emph{Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent}), a hierarchical framework that learns an anticipatory joint-intent interface between language generation and closed-loop control. DAJI-Act distills a future-aware teacher into a deployable diffusion action policy through student-driven rollouts, while DAJI-Flow autoregressively generates future intent chunks from language and intent history. Experiments show that DAJI achieves strong results in anticipatory latent learning, single-instruction generation, and streaming instruction following, reaching 94.42\% rollout success on HumanML3D-style generation and 0.152 subsequence FID on BABEL.

</details>


### [132] [Let Robots Feel Your Touch: Visuo-Tactile Cortical Alignment for Embodied Mirror Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14571)
*Tianfang Zhu,Ning An,Rui Wang,Jiasi Gao,Qingming Luo,Anan Li,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Mirror Touch Net，通过语义、分布和几何对齐约束，实现从RGB图像预测机器人手上1140个触觉单元的毫米级触觉信号，并扩展到跨域人体手部触觉预测，为机器人提供镜像触觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类存在镜像触觉现象（观察他人被触碰时自身产生触觉），这一现象依赖于视觉皮层与体感皮层之间的结构对应。然而，机器人系统缺乏实现该原理的计算框架，无法将视觉-触觉共振的神经机制转化为机器人感知能力。

Method: 提出Mirror Touch Net，通过多层约束（语义对齐、分布对齐、几何对齐）实现视觉与触觉表征之间的对齐，从而从RGB图像预测机器人手上1140个触觉单元的触觉信号。进一步将该对齐框架扩展到跨域人体手部观察，实现对人体触觉的预测和反射式响应。

Result: 该方法能从RGB图像预测毫米级精度的触觉信号；流形分析表明约束重塑了视觉表征，使其几何结构与触觉流形一致，降低了跨模态映射的复杂度；扩展至人体手部观察也能实现触觉预测和反射式响应。

Conclusion: 该工作将视觉-触觉共振的神经原理与机器人感知联系起来，为实现可解释的预期性触觉和共情式人机交互提供了路径。

Abstract: Observing touch on another's body can elicit corresponding tactile sensations in the observer, a phenomenon termed mirror touch that supports empathy and social perception. This visuo-tactile resonance is thought to rely on structural correspondence between visual and somatosensory cortices, yet robotic systems lack computational frameworks that instantiate this principle. Here we demonstrate that cortical correspondence can be operationalized to endow robots with mirror touch. We introduce Mirror Touch Net, which imposes semantic, distributional and geometric alignment between visual and tactile representations through multi-level constraints, enabling prediction of millimetre-scale tactile signals across 1,140 taxels on a robotic hand from RGB images. Manifold analysis reveals that these constraints reshape visual representations into geometry consistent with the tactile manifold, reducing the complexity of cross-modal mapping. Extending this alignment framework to cross-domain observations of human hands enables tactile prediction and reflexive responses to observed human touch. Our results link a neural principle of visuo-tactile resonance to robotic perception, providing an explainable route towards anticipatory touch and empathic human-robot interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/fun0515/Mirror-Touch-Net.

</details>


### [133] [SR-Platform: An Agentic Pipeline for Natural Language-Driven Robot Simulation Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14700)
*Ben Wei Lim,Minh Duc Le,Thang Truong,Thanh Nguyen Canh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SR-Platform 是一个生产部署的智能体系统，能将自然语言描述转换为可执行的 MuJoCo 机器人仿真环境。系统通过 LLM 编排器、资产锻造、布局架构和桥接层四个阶段，在约50秒内生成包含五个物体的场景，显著降低了机器人仿真环境构建的手动工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成机器人仿真环境是仿真驱动机器人学习的主要瓶颈。构建可训练的 MuJoCo 场景通常需要掌握 3D 资产建模、MJCF 规范、空间布局、碰撞避免和机器人模型集成等专业知识，手动构建耗时且门槛高。

Method: SR-Platform 将场景合成分解为四个阶段：(1) LLM 编排器将用户意图转化为结构化场景计划；(2) 资产锻造通过 LLM-to-CadQuery 合成检索缓存资产或生成新 3D 几何；(3) 布局架构分配物体姿态并验证工业约束；(4) 桥接层组装最终 MJCF 场景并融合选定机器人模型。系统部署为九个服务的 Docker 栈，集成 WebSocket、MinIO、Qdrant、Redis 和 InfluxDB 等组件。

Result: 基于30天611次成功 LLM 调用的生产遥测数据，SR-Platform 生成五物体场景的中位端到端延迟约为50秒，缓存加速场景约30-40秒。资产锻造首次尝试重试率为11.3%且可自动恢复，缓存资产检索消除了对已生成物体类型的逐个 LLM 调用。

Conclusion: 智能体场景合成能够减少创建多样化机器人训练环境所需的手动工作量，用户可在不到一分钟内通过自然语言提示生成可执行的 MuJoCo 场景。

Abstract: Generating robot simulation environments remains a major bottleneck in simulation-based robot learning. Constructing a training-ready MuJoCo scene typically requires expertise in 3D asset modeling, MJCF specification, spatial layout, collision avoidance, and robot-model integration. We present SR-Platform, a production-deployed agentic system that converts free-form natural language descriptions into executable, physically valid MuJoCo environments. SR-Platform decomposes scene synthesis into four stages: an LLM-based orchestrator that converts user intent into a structured scene plan; an asset forge that retrieves cached assets or generates new 3D geometry through LLM-to-CadQuery synthesis; a layout architect that assigns object poses and verifies industrial constraints; and a bridge layer that assembles the final MJCF scene and merges the selected robot model. The system is deployed as a nine-service Docker stack with WebSocket progress streaming, MinIO-backed mesh storage, Qdrant-based semantic asset retrieval, Redis job state, and InfluxDB telemetry. Using 30 days of production telemetry covering 611 successful LLM calls, SR-Platform generates five-object scenes with a median end-to-end latency of approximately 50 s, while cache-accelerated scenes complete in approximately 30-40 s. The asset forge shows an 11.3% first-attempt retry rate with automatic recovery, and cached asset retrieval removes per-object LLM calls for previously generated object types. These results show that agentic scene synthesis can reduce the manual effort required to create diverse robot training environments, enabling users to produce executable MuJoCo scenes from plain English prompts in under one minute.

</details>


### [134] [IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Laurence Tianruo Yang,Yurun Jin,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IntentVLA，一种历史条件化的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过编码近期视觉观察为紧凑的短视意图表示来条件化动作块生成，解决了机器人模仿数据中因多模态性导致的块间冲突和不稳定执行问题。同时引入AliasBench基准测试，在多项任务中提升了执行稳定性并优于现有VLA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿数据具有多模态性：相似的视觉-语言观察可能对应不同的动作块，原因是人类演示者具有不同的短视意图、任务阶段或近期上下文。现有的帧条件化VLA策略仅从当前观察和指令推断每个动作块，在部分可观测性下可能在不同重规划步骤间重新采样不同的意图，导致块间冲突和不稳定执行。

Method: 提出IntentVLA，一个历史条件化的VLA框架，编码近期视觉观察为紧凑的短视意图表示，并用该表示条件化动作块生成。同时构建AliasBench，一个包含12个任务的模糊感知基准测试，用于隔离短视观察混淆问题。

Result: 在AliasBench、SimplerEnv、LIBERO和RoboCasa上，IntentVLA提升了 rollout 稳定性，并优于强VLA基线方法。

Conclusion: 历史条件化的意图表示能有效缓解帧条件化VLA策略因部分可观测性导致的块间冲突问题，提升多模态模仿数据下的执行稳定性。

Abstract: Robot imitation data are often multimodal: similar visual-language observations may be followed by different action chunks because human demonstrators act with different short-horizon intents, task phases, or recent context. Existing frame-conditioned VLA policies infer each chunk from the current observation and instruction alone, so under partial observability they may resample different intents across adjacent replanning steps, leading to inter-chunk conflict and unstable execution. We introduce IntentVLA, a history-conditioned VLA framework that encodes recent visual observations into a compact short-horizon intent representation and uses it to condition chunk generation. We further introduce AliasBench, a 12-task ambiguity-aware benchmark on RoboTwin2 with matched training data and evaluation environments that isolate short-horizon observation aliasing. Across AliasBench, SimplerEnv, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, IntentVLA improves rollout stability and outperforms strong VLA baselines

</details>


### [135] [Exploring Bottlenecks in VLM-LLM Navigation: How 3D Scene Understanding Capability Impacts Zero-Shot VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14801)
*Ziyi Xia,Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文量化了3D场景理解能力对零样本视觉-语言导航（VLN）的影响，提出了基于VLM-LLM框架的两个子系统（慢速LLM规划器和快速反应导航器）的统计成功率上界，发现3D感知存在饱和现象——感知精度超过一定阈值后对导航成功率的提升收益递减，建议VLN的3D场景理解应放弃像素级精度，转而关注导航相关的核心词汇和准确的边界框比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前零样本VLN系统将预训练VLM和LLM集成，其中VLM构建3D场景图、LLM进行高层推理决策。但现有3D感知模型追求像素级精度，与体感导航严格的算力限制和实时性需求相冲突，这一关键瓶颈尚未被量化研究。

Method: 基于典型的VLM-LLM框架，提出两个子系统的统计成功率上界：1）依赖拓扑映射语义的慢速LLM规划器；2）利用空间坐标和边界框执行LLM决策的快速反应导航器。使用最先进的3D场景理解模型进行验证。

Result: 验证了所提出的成功率上界，并揭示了感知饱和现象——感知精度超过特定阈值后，对导航成功率的提升呈现边际递减效应。

Conclusion: 3D场景理解用于VLN时应放弃严格的像素级精度，转而优先关注导航相关的核心词汇和准确的边界框比例。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has gained significant attention due to its minimal data collection costs and inherent generalization. This paradigm is typically driven by the integration of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs), where VLMs construct 3D scene graphs while LLMs handle high-level reasoning and decision-making. However, a critical bottleneck exists in this system: current 3D perception models prioritize pixel-level accuracy, directly conflicting with the strict computational limits and real-time efficiency demanded by embodied navigation. To address this gap, this paper quantifies the actual impact of 3D scene understanding capability on VLN performance. Based on typical VLM-LLM frameworks, we propose statistical success rate (SR) upper bounds for two core subsystems: 1) the slow LLM planner, which relies on topological mapping semantics, and 2) the fast reactive navigator, which utilizes spatial coordinates and bounding boxes to execute LLM decisions. Evaluations using state-of-the-art 3D scene understanding models validate our proposed bounds and reveal a perception saturation phenomenon, indicating that improvements in perception accuracy beyond a certain threshold yield diminishing returns in navigation success. Our findings suggest that 3D scene understanding for VLN should pivot away from strict pixel-level precision, prioritizing instead navigation-relevant core vocabularies and accurate bounding box proportions.

</details>


### [136] [Learning Cross-Coupled and Regime Dependent Dynamics for Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14805)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Samaksh Ujjawal,Sihao Sun,Spandan Roy,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对空中机械臂系统中存在的强耦合、时滞气动交互和非平稳动力学变化问题，提出了一种结构化的编码器-解码器框架用于自适应残差动力学学习。非线性潜变量编码器捕获状态-输入历史中的交叉耦合和时间依赖关系，轻量线性潜变量解码器支持在线贝叶斯自适应和协方差驱动膨胀，实现快速稳定的自适应，并与实时模型预测控制兼容。实验验证了该方法在残差预测精度、适应速度和轨迹跟踪性能上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在复杂任务（如载荷运输）中需要精确的动力学模型，但系统存在强四旋翼-机械臂耦合、时滞气动交互以及由载荷变化和机械臂重构导致的非平稳动力学变化。这些效应产生的残差动力学具有交叉耦合、历史依赖和非平稳特性，导致传统解析模型和纯离线学习模型在部署时性能下降。

Method: 提出一种结构化编码器-解码器框架：非线性潜变量编码器从状态-输入历史中捕获交叉变量耦合和时间依赖关系；轻量线性潜变量解码器采用线性参数化结构，支持闭式贝叶斯自适应和一致性驱动的协方差膨胀，实现快速稳定自适应，并兼容实时模型预测控制。

Result: 在真实空中机械臂平台上的实验表明：该方法在残差预测精度、不同工况下的适应速度以及基于MPC的轨迹跟踪性能方面均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模耦合时间动态和部署时的非平稳性对于可靠的空中机械臂操作至关重要，所提框架有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics models are critical for aerial manipulators operating under complex tasks such as payload transport. However, modeling these systems remains fundamentally challenging due to strong quadrotor-manipulator coupling, delayed aerodynamic interactions, and regime-dependent dynamics variations arising from payload changes and manipulator reconfiguration. These effects produce residual dynamics that are simultaneously cross-coupled, history-dependent, and nonstationary, causing both analytical models and purely offline learned models to degrade during deployment. To address these challenges, we propose a structured encoder-decoder framework for adaptive residual dynamics learning in aerial manipulators. The proposed nonlinear latent encoder captures cross-variable coupling and temporal dependencies from state-input histories, while a lightweight linear latent decoder enables online adaptation under regime-dependent nonstationary dynamics. The linear-in-parameter decoder structure permits closed-form Bayesian adaptation together with consistency-driven covariance inflation, enabling rapid and stable adaptation to both transient and slowly varying dynamics changes while remaining compatible with real-time model predictive control (MPC). Experimental results on a real aerial manipulation platform demonstrate improved residual prediction accuracy, faster adaptation under changing operating conditions, and enhanced MPC-based trajectory tracking performance. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling coupled temporal dynamics and deployment-time nonstationarity for reliable aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [137] [CaMeRL: Collision-Aware and Memory-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for UAV Navigation in Multi-Scale Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14810)
*Hong Hong,Feiyu Liao,Yongheng Liang,Boning Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机避障导航中多尺度障碍物问题，提出CaMeRL框架，通过碰撞感知潜在表示（保留小障碍物细节）和时间记忆模块（缓解大障碍物遮挡导致的局部可观测性），在超小和超大障碍物设置下，成功率分别提升0.48和0.28，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机避障方法主要关注障碍物数量或密度，忽视了尺度变化的影响；基于单帧深度图像的几何特征提取会忽略小障碍物，并在大障碍物遮挡下丢失空间上下文，导致在多尺度障碍物环境中性能显著下降。

Method: 提出CaMeRL（Collision-aware and Memory-enhanced Reinforcement Learning）框架，包含两个核心模块：1）碰撞感知潜在表示，编码风险敏感的深度线索以保留细粒度障碍物结构，提升对小障碍物的敏感度；2）时间记忆模块，跨帧整合观测信息，缓解大障碍物遮挡造成的部分可观测性。

Result: 在多尺度障碍物环境（包括超小和超大障碍物设置）下评估，CaMeRL在所有尺度上均优于现有基线方法，在超小和超大障碍物设置中成功率分别提升0.48和0.28；并且能够在杂乱的室外环境中实现可靠的导航。

Conclusion: CaMeRL通过结合碰撞感知编码和时间记忆机制，有效解决了多尺度障碍物场景下的无人机避障导航问题，证明了考虑障碍物尺度变化的重要性。

Abstract: In obstacle avoidance navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), variations in obstacle scale have received strangely less attention than obstacle number or density. Existing methods typically extract purely geometric features from single-frame depth observations. Such representations tend to neglect small obstacles and lose spatial context under occlusions caused by large obstacles, leading to noticeable degradation in environments with multi-scale obstacles. To address this issue, we propose CaMeRL, a Collision-aware and Memory-enhanced Reinforcement Learning framework for UAV navigation. The collision-aware latent representation encodes risk-sensitive depth cues to preserve fine-grained obstacle structures, thereby improving sensitivity to small obstacles. The temporal memory module integrates observations across frames, mitigating partial observability caused by large-obstacle occlusions. We evaluate CaMeRL with multi-scale obstacles, including ultra-small and extra-large obstacle settings. Results show that CaMeRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scales, with success rate gains of 0.48 and 0.28 in the ultra-small and extra-large settings, respectively. More importantly, CaMeRL achieves reliable navigation in cluttered outdoor environments.

</details>


### [138] [Learning Direct Control Policies with Flow Matching for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14832)
*Marcello Ceresini,Federico Pirazzoli,Andrea Bertogalli,Lorenzo Cipelli,Filippo D'Addeo,Anthony Dell'Eva,Alessandro Paolo Capasso,Alberto Broggi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流匹配的运动规划器，能够直接输出加速度和曲率控制轨迹，在BEV场景条件下通过少量ODE积分步生成控制序列，支持实时闭环重规划。仅使用城市合成数据训练，但在包含高速公路等分布外场景中展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶运动规划方法在闭环控制性能与泛化能力之间存在矛盾，且对训练数据分布高度敏感，难以适应分布偏移的场景（如从城市到高速公路的迁移）。

Method: 采用流匹配(flow-matching)框架，以鸟瞰图(BEV)光栅化场景为条件，通过少量常微分方程(ODE)积分步骤直接生成加速度和曲率定义的可执行控制轨迹。模型仅使用包含反应式智能体的2D交通模拟器收集的城市场景数据训练，并在闭环设置下评估。

Result: 在分布内和分布外（多车道高速公路、未见城市场景）环境中均表现出稳定闭环控制，成功完成与训练分布显著不同的场景任务，且推理延迟低，适合实时重规划。

Conclusion: BEV表示提供的几何中心视角对分布偏移不敏感，流匹配公式学习到的平滑向量场在分布偏移下也能优雅退化，两者共同保证了模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a flow-matching planner for autonomous driving that directly outputs actionable control trajectories defined by acceleration and curvature profiles. The model is conditioned on a bird's-eye-view (BEV) raster of the surrounding scene and generates control sequences in a small number of Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) integration steps, enabling low-latency inference suitable for real-time closed-loop re-planning. We train exclusively on urban scenarios (real urban city streets, intersections and roundabouts of the city of Parma, Italy) collected from a 2D traffic simulator with reactive agents, and evaluate in closed-loop on both in-distribution and markedly out-of-distribution environments, including multi-lane highways and unseen urban scenarios. Our results show that the model generalizes reliably to these unseen conditions, maintaining stable closed-loop control and successfully completing scenarios that differ substantially from the training distribution. We attribute this to the BEV representation, which provides a geometry-centric view of the scene that is inherently less sensitive to distributional shifts, and to the flow-matching formulation, which learns a smooth vector field that degrades gracefully under distribution shift. We provide video demonstrations of closed-loop behavior at https://marcelloceresini.github.io/DirectControlFlowMatching.

</details>


### [139] [Chrono-Gymnasium: An Open-Source, Gymnasium-Compatible Distributed Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14911)
*Bocheng Zou,Harry Zhang,Khailanii Slaton,Jingquan Wang,Derrick Ruan,Huzaifa Mustafa Unjhawala,Radu Serban,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Chrono-Gymnasium是一个基于Ray框架的分布式计算平台，用于扩展高保真多体动力学仿真Project Chrono到大规模集群，提供标准Gymnasium接口，支持强化学习和贝叶斯优化等数据密集型任务，在保持物理精度的同时显著减少墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真对缩小仿真到现实的差距至关重要，但其计算开销限制了在强化学习和全局优化等数据密集型任务中的应用，亟需可扩展的分布式仿真框架。

Method: 基于Ray框架构建分布式计算架构，提供标准化Gymnasium接口，集成内置同步和消息传递原语，实现高保真多体动力学仿真（Project Chrono）在集群上的分布式执行。

Result: 在自主机器人导航（强化学习）和行星着陆器设计参数优化（贝叶斯优化）两个案例中，Chrono-Gymnasium在不牺牲物理精度的前提下显著降低了高保真仿真的墙钟时间。

Conclusion: Chrono-Gymnasium为复杂机器人系统的设计与控制提供了一种可扩展的高保真仿真路径，有效弥合了计算效率与物理精度之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: High-fidelity physics simulation is essential for closing the sim-to-real gap in robotics and complex mechanical systems. However, the computational overhead of high-fidelity engines often limits their use in data-intensive tasks like Reinforcement Learning (RL) and global optimization. We introduce Chrono-Gymnasium, a distributed computing framework that scales the high-fidelity multi-body dynamics of Project Chrono across large-scale computing clusters. Built upon the Ray framework, Chrono-Gymnasium provides a standardized Gymnasium interface, enabling seamless integration with modern machine learning libraries while providing built-in synchronization and messaging primitives for distributed execution. We demonstrate the framework's capabilities through two distinct case studies: (1) the training of an RL agent for autonomous robotic navigation in complex terrains, and (2) the Bayesian Optimization of a planetary lander's design parameters to ensure landing stability. Our results show that Chrono-Gymnasium reduces wall-clock time for high-fidelity simulations without sacrificing physical accuracy, offering a scalable path for the design and control of complex robotic systems.

</details>


### [140] [SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15074)
*Johannes Scherer,Sebastian Hirt,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOCC-ICP提出了一种基于语义占据网格的LiDAR里程计框架，将几何与语义信息统一到同一地图表示中，通过自适应ICP和射线投射滤波实现鲁棒的位姿估计，在多种场景下达到SOTA性能，并能为下游任务直接提供语义占据网格地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR里程计方法使用的点、曲面块或NDT地图表示与下游任务（如运动规划）常用的语义占据网格不一致，导致需要维护冗余的地图结构。作者希望统一里程计与语义占据网格建图，消除冗余。

Method: 1) 每个地图体素编码几何和语义统计信息；2) 基于局部平面性自适应选择point-to-point或point-to-plane ICP；3) 通过射线投射的自由空间更新自然过滤动态物体；4) 语义标签可融入地图构建、下采样和对应加权中。

Result: 在多种评估场景中，SOCC-ICP达到了与SOTA LiDAR里程计竞争的性能；在几何退化环境中依然鲁棒（即使没有语义线索）；引入语义标签后准确度进一步提升。

Conclusion: SOCC-ICP通过单一表示统一了里程计与语义占据网格建图，消除了冗余地图结构，直接为下游机器人应用提供可用的地图，同时保持高精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [141] [CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15120)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLOVER提出了一种闭环价值估计与排序框架，通过生成器-评分器架构、伪专家轨迹扩展、闭环自蒸馏等策略，解决端到端自动驾驶规划中模仿学习与规则评估不匹配的问题，在NAVSIM等基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶规划器通常通过模仿单一日志轨迹进行训练，但评估时采用基于规则的规划指标（如安全性、可行性、进度、舒适度），导致训练与评估之间存在不匹配：接近日志路径的轨迹可能违反规划规则，而远离演示轨迹的替代方案可能有效且得分更高。这种不匹配对于候选-选择型规划器尤为突出。

Method: CLOVER采用轻量级生成器-评分器框架：(1) 生成器产生多样化的候选轨迹；(2) 评分器预测规划指标子分数以在推理时排序。具体包括：通过评估器过滤构建伪专家轨迹，用集合级覆盖监督训练生成器；执行保守闭环自蒸馏，使评分器拟合真实评估子分数，生成器向教师选择的top-k和向量帕累托目标优化并加入稳定性正则化。

Result: NAVSIM上达到94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，刷新SOTA；更具挑战性的NavHard上取得48.3 EPDMS，匹配最强报告结果；nuScenes开环评估中取得最低L2误差和碰撞率。

Conclusion: CLOVER通过闭环价值估计与排序框架成功弥合了模仿学习训练与规则评估之间的鸿沟，证明了评分器介导的生成器改进在保守更新条件下是可靠的。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision.
