### [162] [Ready from Day 1: Population-Aware Coordination for Large-Scale Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13900)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Carson Eisenach,Dean Foster*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种群体感知协调接口方法，通过学习条件化的原始映射和对偶映射来预测大规模多智能体系统在资源规划中的利用率和成本轨迹，从而在群体组成变化时无需重新训练即可保持可靠性，并支持从少量样本协调大规模群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模多智能体系统中，上游规划者需要迭代评估候选资源计划，但成本-利用率响应映射依赖于随规划周期变化的群体组成，传统的拉格朗日松弛方法无法适应这种动态变化。

Method: 提出群体感知协调接口，包括条件化于紧凑群体摘要的原始映射（预测给定成本轨迹下的总利用率）和对偶映射（预测目标计划对应的成本轨迹）；支持Sim2Real回测流程进行部署前评估。

Result: 在供应链容量控制案例中，群体感知接口相较基线降低16-19%的预测误差和20-51%的容量违规；2万智能体样本可准确协调50万智能体群体；模拟训练的原始映射在真实数据上达到11.1% MAPE，显著优于基线（13-24%）。

Conclusion: 群体感知协调接口通过编码响应相关的群体结构，实现了跨动态群体组成的可靠协调，无需每周期重新训练，且支持从子样本扩展到大规模群体的协调。

Abstract: In large-scale multi-agent systems with shared resource constraints, an upstream planner must iteratively evaluate candidate resource plans -- assessing feasibility, aggregate response, and marginal cost -- before committing to one. Lagrangian relaxation separates local decisions through a broadcast cost signal, but the planner still needs the cost-to-utilization response map to explore plan space, and this map depends on population composition that changes across planning cycles. We propose \emph{population-aware coordination interfaces}: learned primal and dual maps, conditioned on compact population summaries, that the planner queries inside its iterative loop. The primal map predicts aggregate utilization under a proposed cost trajectory; the dual map predicts the cost trajectory for a target plan. By encoding response-relevant population structure, these maps remain reliable across evolving populations without per-cycle retraining, and support coordination of large populations from compact subsamples. We additionally cast Sim2Real transfer as a backtestable procedure, enabling evaluation before deployment. In a supply-chain capacity-control case study, population-aware interfaces reduce forecast error by 16--19\% and capacity violations by 20--51\% relative to population-unaware baselines under composition shift; 20K-agent cohorts support accurate coordination of 500K-agent populations; and simulator-trained primal maps achieve 11.1\% MAPE on real observations versus 13--24\% for baselines.

</details>


### [163] [Decision-Level Fusion for Robust Wearable Affect Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14878)
*Lokesh Singh,Athina Georgara,Jayati Deshmukh,Tan Viet Tuyen Nguyen,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于非平稳生理信号处理的多模态情感识别方法，使用FBSE-EWT提取时变特征，并通过决策级融合（基于不确定性+模态可靠性加权）提升对异质传感的鲁棒性，在WESAD上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴情感识别的实际部署面临生理信号非平稳动态、伪影及传感器缺失等挑战，而传统固定基谱特征（如FFT频带功率、Welch PSD）会过度平滑短时判别模式，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出非平稳处理流程：1）利用Fourier-Bessel级数展开（FBSE）结合经验小波变换（EWT）进行数据驱动的谱分割，提取模态级瞬态描述子；2）采用决策级融合（而非特征级）聚合各模态预测器，并基于预测不确定性与模态可靠性进行加权。

Result: 在WESAD数据集（15名受试者，ECG/EDA/BVP/EMG/ACC五模态，三分类）上，决策级融合约84%时间不劣于特征级融合，约48%时间严格更优，表明在异质且部分不可靠传感条件下鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 面向非平稳生理信号的情感识别中，基于FBSE-EWT的时变特征提取与不确定性加权的决策级融合能有效应对动态变化与传感退化，为实际可穿戴部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic recognition of affective state from wearable physiology has clear societal impact for public health, preventive care, and stress-aware interventions, but real deployments require robustness to non-stationary dynamics, artefacts, and missing sensors. We study this problem on WESAD, using baseline, stress, and amusement conditions, where common fixed-basis spectral features such as FFT bandpower and Welch PSD can oversmooth short-lived discriminative patterns. We propose a non-stationary pipeline that combines Fourier-Bessel Series Expansion (FBSE) with EWT data-driven spectral segmentation to extract mode-wise transient descriptors. For multimodal integration, we adopt decision-level aggregation over per-modality predictors and weight each modality by predictive uncertainty and modality reliability. Results on WESAD, using 15 subjects and ECG, EDA, BVP, EMG, and ACC signals across three classes, indicate that decision-level aggregation is approximately 84 percent of the time at least as good as feature-level aggregation, and approximately 48 percent of the time strictly better, suggesting improved robustness under heterogeneous and partially reliable sensing.

</details>


### [164] [Temporal Fair Division in Multi-Agent Systems: From Precise Alternation Metrics to Scalable Coordination Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14879)
*Nikolaos Al. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了旋转周期性（RP）和ALT两类时序公平性度量指标，在多智能体重复资源竞争场景中建立时序公平理论，并通过实验证明传统指标无法揭示的协调失败问题，RP相比ALT实现12-25倍的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中智能体需在重复交互中竞争有限资源，需要一种能够评判整个交互历史时序公平性的理论框架，而传统公平性指标无法捕捉时序维度上的不公平现象。

Method: 形式化定义多智能体重复公平分配实例MBoE，提出RP家族指标（含RS和WPE两个子度量，复杂度O(νn+n)）和ALT滑动窗口指标家族（复杂度O(νn²)），通过实验对比两者性能。

Result: (1) Q-learning智能体在RP和CALT上比随机策略差10-73%和7-35%，而Reward Fairness仍高于0.92，显示传统指标失效；(2) RP比ALT快12-25倍，加速比随n增长；(3) 两者互补：ALT在小群体中区分度更高，RP在ALT不可行时仍能可靠扩展。

Conclusion: RP和ALT共同构成了时序公平分配的诊断工具包，能揭示传统指标无法发现的协调失败问题，为多智能体重复资源竞争提供了有效的时序公平性评估框架。

Abstract: A plethora real-world environments require agents to compete repeatedly for the same limited resource, calling for a temporal notion of fairness judged across entire interaction histories. This paper advances the theory of temporal fair division by introducing Rotational Periodicity (RP), a family of lightweight metrics, alongside the ALT family of sliding-window measures, within a unified framework for repeated multi-agent resource competition.
  We formalise the Multi-Agent Battle of the Exes (MBoE) as a repeated fair division instance and establish Perfect Alternation (PA) as its canonical temporally fair solution, drawing connections to proportionality, envy-freeness, and n-periodic round-robin allocation. RP decomposes temporal fairness into two complementary sub-measures: Rotational Score (RS) and Waiting Periods Evaluation (WPE), achieving O(nu+n) time complexity versus the O(nu*n) of ALT, where nu is the episode count and n the agent count.
  Empirical evaluation across n in {2,3,5,8,10} reveals three findings. First, both RP and ALT expose a coordination failure invisible to traditional metrics: Q-learning agents perform worse than random policies by 10-73% on RP and 7-35% on CALT, while Reward Fairness remains misleadingly high (above 0.92 for n>=3). Second, RP achieves 12-25x computational speedup over ALT, growing with n. Third, the two families are complementary: ALT provides richer discrimination for small populations; RP scales reliably where ALT becomes intractable. Together they form a diagnostic toolkit for temporal fair division.

</details>


### [165] [Multi-Agentic Approach for History Matching of Oil Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15028)
*Linar Samigullin,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PetroGraph是一个多智能体框架，通过将油藏历史匹配工作流分解为多个专业化智能体（模型审查、实验规划、参数化、优化、模拟和总结），结合大语言模型、领域工具、检索增强文档、人工审核和OPM Flow模拟后端，实现自然语言驱动的自动化历史匹配。在SPE1、SPE9和Norne三个油藏模型上分别降低了95%、69%和13%的加权归一化均方根误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史匹配作为油藏工程中的核心反问题，虽然自动化方法可以减少人工工作量，但由于工程师仍需配置包括参数选择、物理边界、优化器选择、超参数调优、模拟执行和诊断报告在内的异构工作流，实际部署仍然困难。因此需要一个智能框架来降低专家门槛并自动化关键决策。

Method: 提出PetroGraph多智能体框架，将历史匹配工作流分解为模型审查、实验规划、参数化、优化、模拟和总结六个专业化智能体；集成大语言模型、检索增强的模拟器文档访问、ECLIPSE输入文件验证、人在回路检查点以及OPM Flow模拟后端；支持用户通过自然语言启动和引导历史匹配过程，同时保留对选定参数和优化设置的显式控制。

Result: 在三个复杂度递增的油藏模型上评估：合成SPE1模型降低95%的加权归一化均方根误差，断层SPE9基准模型降低69%，真实油田Norne模型降低13%。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排能够自动化历史匹配中的关键决策，降低复杂模拟工作流的操作门槛，并为可扩展的、领域感知的油藏模型适配提供灵活基础。

Abstract: History matching is a central inverse problem in reservoir engineering, where uncertain reservoir parameters must be calibrated against observations. Although automated history matching can reduce manual effort, practical deployment remains difficult because engineers must still configure heterogeneous workflows involving parameter selection, physically admissible bounds, optimizer choice, hyperparameter tuning, simulator execution, and diagnostic reporting. We propose PetroGraph, a multi-agent framework for intelligent reservoir history matching that decomposes this workflow into specialized agents for model review, experimental planning, parameterization, optimization, simulation, and summarization. The system combines large language model agents with domain-specific tools, retrieval-augmented access to simulator documentation, validation of modified ECLIPSE input decks, human-in-the-loop checkpoints, and an OPM Flow-based simulation backend. This design enables users to initiate and steer history matching through natural language while preserving explicit control over selected parameters and optimization settings. We evaluate PetroGraph on three reservoir models of increasing complexity: the synthetic SPE1 model, the faulted SPE9 benchmark, and the real-field Norne model. Using weighted normalized root mean square error as the objective, PetroGraph reduces the mismatch by 95% on SPE1, 69% on SPE9, and 13% on Norne. These results demonstrate that multi-agent orchestration can automate key decisions in history matching, lower the expertise barrier for operating complex simulation workflows, and provide a flexible foundation for extensible, domain-aware reservoir model adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Neural Code Translation of Legacy Code: APL to C#](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13896)
*Abdulrahman Ramadan,Hanen Borchani,Iben Lilholm,Mikkel Almind,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大语言模型将高度简洁的APL语言自动翻译为C#语言，构建了多组功能等价代码对数据集，提出了三种引导策略（自然语言描述中介、检索增强和迭代优化）并与基线直接翻译模型对比，开发了自动化评估流水线验证语法编译和功能执行，结果表明神经代码翻译能有效桥接APL与C#，引入额外上下文和引导显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程语言之间的自动翻译仍具挑战性，尤其当源语言（如APL）具有高度简洁性和专业性时。APL的稀疏语法、大规模平行语料稀缺以及需要专业知识来解释APL程序，使得这一翻译任务尤为困难。

Method: 提出了一个APL到C#翻译的新框架，比较了三种引导策略：自然语言描述中介、检索增强和迭代优化，与基线直接翻译模型进行对比。构建了涵盖不同复杂度水平的多组功能等价代码对数据集，并开发了自动化评估流水线来验证生成C#代码的语法编译和功能执行。

Result: 神经代码翻译能够成功桥接APL与C#之间的差距，覆盖广泛程序；引入额外上下文和引导策略显著提升了模型翻译性能。

Conclusion: 引入额外上下文和引导策略能够有效提升从APL到C#的神经代码翻译质量，验证了该方法在多种复杂度程序上的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic translation between programming languages remains a challenging problem, particularly when the source language is highly concise and specialized. This paper investigates the translation of APL into C# using large language models. The task is difficult due to APL's sparse syntax, the scarcity of large-scale parallel corpora, and the requirement for specialized knowledge to interpret APL programs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework for APL-to-C# translation by comparing three guided strategies, namely natural language description-mediated, retrieval-augmented, and iterative refinement, against a baseline direct translation model. We constructed multiple datasets of functionally equivalent code pairs spanning various levels of complexity, and to rigorously assess translation quality, we developed an automated evaluation pipeline that verifies both syntactic compilation and functional execution of the generated C# code. Our results demonstrate that neural code translation can successfully bridge the gap between APL and C# for a wide range of programs, and that incorporating additional context and guidance significantly improves model performance.

</details>


### [167] [Bidirectional Empowerment of Metamorphic Testing and Large Language Models: A Systematic Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13898)
*Zheng Zheng,Zenghui Zhou,Yinwang Xu,Daixu Ren,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了93篇研究，提出元测试（MT）与大语言模型（LLM）之间双向赋能的观点：一方面MT用于验证和评估LLM系统的各类问题（如幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性等），另一方面LLM用于自动化和增强MT的各个环节（如蜕变关系发现、输入变换、测试实现等），建立了结构化的知识体系并展望了未来AI质量保证方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型因其生成性、概率性和开放性给软件质量保证带来巨大挑战，传统测试方法面临Oracle问题（无法确定预期输出）的限制。元测试通过检查多次执行之间的必要关系而不依赖精确期望输出，成为测试LLM等Oracle缺陷系统的有效方法；同时LLM的语义理解和代码生成能力也为自动化MT的传统劳动密集型阶段创造了新机遇。

Method: 对93篇主要研究进行系统综述，提出MT与LLM双向赋能关系的分类体系，涵盖两个互补方向：（1）MT for LLMs——使用MT验证、评估和理解LLM及其系统，涉及幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性、代码可靠性、检索增强生成、对话和自主智能体等问题；（2）LLMs for MT——利用LLM支持蜕变关系发现、输入变换与合成、可执行测试实现及智能体闭环测试。

Result: 构建了MT与LLM双向赋能的结构化分类体系，系统总结了MT在LLM各问题领域中的应用，以及LLM如何自动化MT各阶段，揭示了二者之间不断发展的协同关系。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解MT与LLM之间的协同演进提供了结构化基础，并指出了构建更严谨、可扩展和可信赖的AI质量保证方法的未来方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have introduced substantial challenges to software quality assurance due to their generative, probabilistic, and open-ended nature, which intensifies the oracle problem and limits the applicability of traditional testing methods. Metamorphic testing (MT), which checks necessary relations among multiple related executions rather than relying on exact expected outputs, has emerged as a promising approach for testing LLMs and other oracle-deficient systems. At the same time, the strong semantic understanding, reasoning, and code generation capabilities of LLMs create new opportunities to automate the traditionally labor-intensive phases of MT. This survey systematically reviews 93 primary studies and characterizes this reciprocal relationship as the bidirectional empowerment of MT and LLMs. We propose a taxonomy spanning two complementary directions: MT for LLMs, which uses MT to verify, validate, assess, and understand LLMs and LLM-based systems across issues such as hallucination, fairness, robustness, code reliability, retrieval-augmented generation, dialogue, and autonomous agents; and LLMs for MT, which leverages LLMs to support metamorphic relation discovery, input transformation and synthesis, executable test implementation, and agentic closed-loop testing. By synthesizing these developments, this survey provides a structured foundation for understanding the evolving synergy between MT and LLMs and highlights future directions for building more rigorous, scalable, and trustworthy AI quality assurance methodologies.

</details>


### [168] [A Non-Destructive Methodological Framework for Modernizing Legacy Clinical Reporting Systems for AI-Driven Pharmacoinformatics: A SAS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13905)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种非破坏性的元数据层框架，在不修改遗留代码的前提下实现临床报表系统的AI就绪，在558个组件的SAS报表库上验证，现代核心代码减少92%，Phase III研究的14种报表类型平均单元级一致性82.7%，最佳达99.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床报表系统虽编码了监管级逻辑，但输出不透明且缺乏机器可读中间层，阻碍AI集成。现有现代化方案要么完全重写，要么渐进式重构但保留结构性障碍。

Method: 构建非破坏性元数据层框架，包含桥接映射、类型化中间表示(IR)和编排器，包装现有组件并将其输出重新暴露为LLM可消费的结构化数据；支持选择性渐进式整合，替换遗留组件为核心例程。

Result: 在558个组件、37.3万行代码的SAS报表库上验证：共存模式下立即实现AI就绪；现代核心减少92%专有代码；Phase III研究14种报表类型中11个达到80%以上单元级一致性（均值82.7%，最佳99.2%）；CDISC CDISCPilot01数据上5个报表均达100%一致性；LLM实验证实IR支持自动化药物警戒、表格摘要和试验配置生成。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条符合监管要求的、通往AI集成临床报表的路径，可在不中断监管提交的前提下加速药物开发。

Abstract: Drug development and pharmacovigilance are frequently bottlenecked by legacy clinical reporting pipelines. These monolithic systems encode regulatory-grade logic but resist AI integration by producing opaque output with no machine-readable intermediate layer. Existing modernization approaches force a choice between full rewrites and incremental refactoring that preserves structural barriers. We present a non-destructive methodological framework achieving AI-driven pharmacoinformatics readiness without altering legacy source code. A metadata layer--comprising a bridge map, a typed Intermediate Representation (IR), and an orchestrator--wraps existing components and re-exposes their outputs as structured data consumable by LLMs. It enables optional incremental consolidation, replacing selected legacy components with metadata-configured core routines while the remainder operates unchanged. Validated on a 558-component SAS reporting library (373,000 lines of code), the framework demonstrated immediate AI-readiness under coexistence mode, yielding machine-readable output. Where consolidation was elected, the modernized core achieved a 92% reduction in proprietary code. Parity validation on 14 report types from a Phase III study achieved cell-level parity of 80% or above on 11 reports (mean 82.7%, best 99.2%). A benchmark using CDISC CDISCPilot01 data achieved 100% parity across 5 reports. LLM experiments confirmed the IR enables automated pharmacovigilance, table summarization, and trial configuration generation. The framework offers a regulation-aware path to AI-integrated clinical reporting, accelerating drug development without interrupting regulatory submissions.

</details>


### [169] [CRANE: Constrained Reasoning Injection for Code Agents via Nullspace Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14084)
*Mingzhi Zhu,Michele Merler,Raju Pavuluri,Stacy Patterson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CRANE方法，通过无训练的参数编辑技术，将Thinking模型的强规划和恢复能力注入Instruct模型，在不牺牲工具使用规范性的前提下提升代码智能体性能。在Roo-Eval、SWE-bench-Verified、Terminal-Bench v2等基准上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体的Instruct模型工具使用规范但规划能力弱，Thinking模型规划能力强但过度思考损害性能，两者互补但存在对齐问题。需要一种方法将Thinking的推理能力注入Instruct，同时保持其工具使用规范性。

Method: CRANE是一种无训练的参数编辑方法，核心包括：1）将Thinking-Instruct参数差作为候选推理编辑池；2）幅度阈值去噪；3）Conservative Taylor Gate保留对推理迁移和工具使用保持共同有益的编辑；4）Graduated Sigmoidal Projection抑制破坏格式的关键方向。

Result: Roo-Eval：Qwen3-30B-A3B达66.2%（+19.5%），Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B达81.5%（+8.7%）；SWE-bench-Verified：两个尺度分别解决122/500和180/500，各多解决14个实例；Terminal-Bench v2：pass1/pass5提升达2.3%/7.8%，分别达7.6%/17.9%和14.8%/30.3%。

Conclusion: CRANE通过零样本参数编辑成功融合Instruct与Thinking模型的互补优势，证明无需额外训练即可大幅提升代码智能体性能，且优于其他模型合并策略。

Abstract: Code agents must both reason over long-horizon repository state and obey strict tool-use protocols. In paired Instruct/Thinking checkpoints, these capabilities are complementary but misaligned. The Instruct model is concise and tool-disciplined, whereas the Thinking model offers stronger planning and recovery behavior but often over-deliberates and degrades agent performance. We present CRANE (Constrained Reasoning Injection for Code Agents via Nullspace Editing), a training-free parameter-editing method that treats the Thinking-Instruct delta as a directional pool of candidate reasoning edits for the Instruct backbone. CRANE combines magnitude thresholding to denoise the delta, a Conservative Taylor Gate to retain edits that are jointly beneficial for reasoning transfer and tool-use preservation, and Graduated Sigmoidal Projection to suppress format-critical update directions. By merging paired Instruct and Thinking checkpoints, CRANE delivers strong gains over either individual model while preserving Instruct-level efficiency: on Roo-Eval it achieves pass1 of 66.2% (+19.5%) for Qwen3-30B-A3B and 81.5% (+8.7%) for Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B; on SWE-bench-Verified it resolves up to 14 additional instances at both scales (122/500 and 180/500); and on Terminal-Bench v2 it improves pass1/pass5 by up to 2.3%/7.8%, reaching 7.6%/17.9% and 14.8%/30.3%, respectively, consistently outperforming alternative merging strategies across all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [170] [LLM-Based Robustness Testing of Microservice Applications: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14202)
*Hrushitha Goud Tigulla,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验，研究了7种提示策略和3种开源LLM（14B-70B参数）在微服务API鲁棒性测试中的应用，发现提示策略比模型规模更能影响测试多样性，并提出GuidedFewShot策略可在单次运行中覆盖最多故障模式（5/9和8/14），同时保持低跨模型相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务API中的畸形、缺失或边界值输入可能级联传播，威胁系统可靠性。鲁棒性测试通过系统性地使用此类输入来暴露服务端故障，但如何生成多样化且有效的测试仍具挑战。LLM虽可从API规范生成测试，但不同模型和提示策略是否产生多样化故障集或收敛于相同故障尚不清楚。

Method: 设计了控制实验，采用7种提示策略（包括Structured、Guided、GuidedFewShot等），应用于3个开源LLM（参数规模14B-70B），针对两个架构不同的微服务系统（Java单语言6服务9故障模式，多语言27服务14故障模式），共执行38次有效运行，生成663个测试用例。

Result: 提示策略比模型规模更能解释多样性差异；Structured提示完全消除多样性；同一模型使用三种不同提示策略在一个系统上实现了完全的故障模式覆盖，优于任何固定提示下的多模型集成。GuidedFewShot在单次运行中达到最高覆盖率（5/9和8/14），同时保持低跨模型相似度。

Conclusion: 仅靠分类规则不足：LLM在没有具体示例的情况下无法区分key-absent和value-empty变异。GuidedFewShot通过将先前鲁棒性测试研究的变异分类法作为领域上下文嵌入，显著提升了测试效果。研究结果在两个系统上均得到复现。

Abstract: Malformed, missing, or boundary-value inputs in microservice APIs can cascade across dependent services, threatening reliability. Robustness testing systematically exercises such inputs to expose server-side failures, but generating diverse, effective tests remains challenging. Large Language Models can generate such tests from API specifications; however, it is unknown whether different models and prompt strategies produce diverse failure sets or converge on the same failures. We report a controlled experiment applying 7 prompt strategies to 3 open-source LLMs (14B-70B parameters) targeting 2 architecturally distinct microservice systems: one Java monolingual (6 services, 9 failure modes) and one polyglot (27 services, 14 failure modes), yielding 38 valid runs and 663 generated tests. We find that prompt strategy explains more variation in diversity than model size: a Structured prompt collapses diversity entirely, while a single model varied across three prompt strategies achieves complete failure-mode coverage on one system, outperforming any multi-model ensemble under a fixed prompt. We introduce two strategies, Guided and GuidedFewShot, that embed a mutation taxonomy from prior robustness testing research as domain context. GuidedFewShot achieves the highest single-run coverage on both systems (5 of 9 and 8 of 14 failure modes) while maintaining low cross-model similarity. A key lesson is that taxonomy rules alone are insufficient: LLMs cannot distinguish key-absent from value-empty mutations without concrete examples. Findings replicate across both systems.

</details>


### [171] [Failure-Guided Fuzzing for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14219)
*Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向混合量子-经典（HQC）程序的失败引导式模糊测试方法，通过两阶段策略（先搜索非收敛种子，再对种子附近的电路参数进行局部模糊测试）来暴露VQE和QAOA程序中的配置故障。实验表明，失败引导的局部模糊测试是提升测试效果的主要驱动力，而符号化种子发现的收益因工作负载而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典算法（如VQE、QAOA）在近期量子计算中至关重要，但现有基于采样的模糊测试在有限执行预算下难以覆盖故障易发区域（即经典优化器超参数与量子电路参数的联合空间），因此需要更高效的测试方法。

Method: 将混合输入建模为经典优化器超参数与量子电路参数的二元组，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段搜索非收敛种子，第二阶段在种子附近对电路参数进行局部模糊测试。对比了五种策略：随机混合测试、经典枚举（无模糊）、随机种子局部模糊、枚举种子局部模糊、符号化种子局部模糊。在Qiskit框架下对VQE实例和QAOA MaxCut实例进行实现与评估。

Result: 失败引导的局部模糊测试是相对于随机测试提升效果的主要来源；符号化种子发现对VQE有额外收益，但在QAOA上表现不稳定。

Conclusion: 复用失败信息是HQC测试的一个有前景的方向，但符号化种子发现的价值取决于具体工作负载。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical (HQC) algorithms, such as the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), are central to near-term quantum computing but remain challenging to test. Sampling-based fuzzing can expose faulty or non-convergent configurations, but under realistic execution budgets, it may miss failure-prone regions in the joint space of classical optimizer settings and quantum circuit parameters.
  This paper studies failure-guided fuzzing for HQC programs. It models a hybrid input as a pair of classical optimizer hyperparameters and quantum circuit parameters, and evaluates a two-phase strategy that first searches for non-convergent seeds and then locally fuzzes circuit parameters around those seeds. To understand where the gains come from, five budgeted strategies are compared: random hybrid testing, classical enumeration without fuzzing, random-seed local fuzzing, enumeration-seed local fuzzing, and concolic-seed local fuzzing. The study is implemented on a VQE instance and a QAOA MaxCut instance in Qiskit. The results show that failure-guided local fuzzing is the main driver of improvement over random testing, while concolic seed discovery provides additional benefits on VQE but is less stable on QAOA. These findings suggest that reusing failure information is a promising direction for HQC testing, but that the value of concolic seed discovery is workload-dependent.

</details>


### [172] [Making OpenAPI Documentation Agent-Ready: Detecting Documentation and REST Smells with a Multi-Agent LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14312)
*Rayfran Rocha Lima,Davi G. Assunção Pinheiro,Thiago Medeiros de Menezes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对企业将REST API通过MCP协议暴露给AI Agent使用时出现的系统性失败，通过对16个生产API（约600个端点）进行大规模实证评估，发现根本原因在于OpenAPI文档存在大量缺陷（2450个smells），而非单纯模型能力不足。开发了多智能体LLM系统Hermes进行自动化检测，最终促使组织调整了AI Agent采纳策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在将现有REST API通过MCP（Model Context Protocol）暴露给AI Agent使用时，发现任务规划、工具选择和负载构建出现系统性失败。已有观点倾向于归因于模型能力不足，但本文作者怀疑问题根源可能在于API文档本身的质量缺陷，因此需要系统性地评估底层API文档的语义完备性。

Method: 提出Hermes——一个基于多智能体LLM的系统，在端点级别检测文档相关和REST相关smells，并生成可解释的诊断报告。在包含16个生产API、约600个端点的工业生态系统中进行大规模评估。

Result: 在600个端点中识别出2,450个smells，所有被分析的操作均存在缺陷。实践者验证确认了检测问题的高一致性，同时在修复决策中揭示了上下文相关的权衡。

Conclusion: 微服务环境中的结构有效性的API并不保证其对Agent消费的语义就绪性。组织据此修订了采纳策略，优先进行选择性端点适配、重新定义文档标准，并将自动化文档评估纳入API治理流程。本文展示了系统性工件评估可作为战略决策支持机制，降低技术风险并指导工业软件生态中的证据驱动的AI采纳。

Abstract: The growing adoption of AI agents and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) has motivated organizations to expose existing REST APIs as agent-consumable tools. In our industrial context, this initiative targeted an ecosystem of 16 production APIs comprising approximately 600 endpoints. Although these APIs were stable and widely used within a microservice architecture, early proof-of-concept experiments revealed systematic failures in task planning, tool selection, and payload construction when accessed through MCP-based agents. Rather than attributing these failures to model limitations alone, we conducted an ecosystem-scale empirical assessment of the underlying OpenAPI documentation. We developed Hermes, a multi-agent LLM-based system that detects documentation and REST-related smells at the endpoint level and generates explainable diagnostic reports. The large-scale evaluation identified 2,450 smells across 600 endpoints, with deficiencies present in all analyzed operations. Practitioner validation confirmed high agreement with the detected issues while also revealing contextual trade-offs in remediation decisions. The findings suggested that structural validity within microservice environments does not guarantee semantic readiness for agent-based consumption. Based on this evidence, the organization revised its adoption strategy, prioritizing selective endpoint adaptation, redefining documentation standards, and integrating automated documentation assessment into API governance workflows. This case illustrates how systematic artifact-level evaluation can function as a strategic decision-support mechanism, reducing technological risk and guiding evidence-based AI adoption in industrial software ecosystems.

</details>


### [173] [FuzzAgent: Multi-Agent System for Evolutionary Library Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14431)
*Yunlong Lyu,Peng Chen,Fengyi Wu,Junzhe Yu,Kit Long Hon,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FuzzAgent，一个多智能体系统，将库模糊测试转化为迭代进化过程。通过专业智能体团队协作，利用运行时证据不断优化测试套件，在20个真实C/C++库上覆盖179619个分支，比四个基线方法提高45.1%-191.2%，发现102个真实库漏洞，其中78个已被上游维护者确认修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库模糊测试对软件供应链安全至关重要，但大规模采用成本高昂：环境搭建繁琐、测试生成难以满足复杂API约束、难以区分真实漏洞与测试引起的崩溃。现有基于LLM的系统多为一次性代码生成器，忽略运行时反馈，限制了代码深度和漏洞有效性。

Method: FuzzAgent采用多智能体架构，将库模糊测试建模为进化过程。多个专业智能体在全生命周期协作，每个决策基于具体的运行时证据（如覆盖率、崩溃信息），通过多轮迭代不断精化测试套件，实现更深覆盖和更高保真的崩溃分析。

Result: 在20个真实C/C++库上，覆盖179619个分支，超过OSS-Fuzz 45.1%、PromptFuzz 73.2%、PromeFuzz 92.1%、OSS-Fuzz-Gen 191.2%。发现102个真实库漏洞，78个已被上游维护者确认修复。

Conclusion: FuzzAgent展示了将库模糊测试从一次性代码生成转变为基于运行时反馈的迭代进化过程的有效性，无需人工干预即可完成全生命周期，显著提升了覆盖率和真实漏洞发现能力。

Abstract: Library fuzzing is essential for hardening the software supply chain, but adopting it at scale remains expensive. Practitioners still spend substantial effort on environment setup, struggle to generate harnesses that respect intricate API constraints, and lack reliable means to tell genuine library bugs from harness-induced crashes. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet they typically operate as one-shot code generators that ignore runtime feedback, which limits both the depth of code they reach and the validity of the bugs they report. We argue that effective library fuzzing is iterative by nature: each campaign exposes new coverage bottlenecks and crashes, and the next campaign should evolve from these signals rather than restart from scratch. Building on this insight, we present FuzzAgent, a multi-agent system that turns library fuzzing into an evolutionary process, in which a team of specialized agents collaborates over the full fuzzing lifecycle and grounds every decision in concrete runtime evidence, so that the harness suite is successively refined toward deeper coverage and higher-fidelity crash analysis across rounds.
  We evaluate FuzzAgent on 20 real-world C/C++ libraries against four state-of-the-art baselines (OSS-Fuzz, OSS-Fuzz-Gen, PromptFuzz, and PromeFuzz). FuzzAgent completes the full fuzzing lifecycle for all 20 libraries without human intervention and reaches 179619 branches, exceeding OSS-Fuzz, PromptFuzz, PromeFuzz, and OSS-Fuzz-Gen by 45.1%, 73.2%, 92.1%, and 191.2%, respectively. FuzzAgent also identifies 102 genuine library bugs, 78 of which have already been acknowledged and fixed by upstream maintainers.

</details>


### [174] [When Retrieval Hurts Code Completion: A Diagnostic Study of Stale Repository Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14478)
*Haojun Weng,Qianqian Yang,Hao Fu,Haobin Pan,Xinwei Lv*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在代码生成的检索增强场景中，过时的仓库上下文片段并非无害噪声，而是会主动诱导模型生成与当前状态不兼容的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究检索到的过时代码片段在检索增强代码生成中究竟是无害噪声，还是会导致模型生成与当前仓库状态不兼容的代码。

Method: 从五个Python仓库中精选17个生产级辅助函数签名变更样本，在屏蔽提交新鲜度和预期签名的提示下，对比仅当前、仅过时、无检索以及混合当前/过时检索四种条件。

Result: 在屏蔽提示下，仅过时检索在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上导致15/17样本生成过时引用（+88.2%），在gpt-4.1-mini上导致13/17样本（+76.5%）；无检索产生零过时引用但仅1/17通过；两模型过时触发样本Jaccard重叠75.0%；混合条件中有效当前证据可大幅挽救过时失败。

Conclusion: 检索上下文的时效性是Code RAG鲁棒性的独立诊断变量：过时上下文会主动将模型偏见引导至过时仓库状态，而非仅仅移除有效证据。

Abstract: Context: Retrieval-augmented code generation relies on cross-file repository context, but retrieved snippets may come from obsolete project states.
  Objectives: We study whether temporally stale repository snippets act as harmless noise or actively induce current-state-incompatible code.
  Methods: We conduct a controlled diagnostic study on a curated 17-sample set of production-helper signature changes from five Python repositories. For each sample, we compare current-only, stale-only, no-retrieval, and mixed current/stale retrieval conditions under prompts that hide commit freshness and expected current signatures.
  Results: Under neutralized prompts, stale-only retrieval induces stale helper references on 15/17 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct samples and 13/17 gpt-4.1-mini samples, corresponding to 88.2 and 76.5 percentage-point increases over current-only retrieval. No retrieval produces zero stale references but only 1/17 passing completions. The two models share 75.0% Jaccard overlap among stale-triggering samples, and mixed conditions show that adding valid current evidence largely rescues stale-only failures.
  Conclusion: Temporal validity of retrieved repository context is a distinct diagnostic variable for Code RAG robustness: stale context can actively bias models toward obsolete repository state rather than merely removing useful evidence.

</details>


### [175] [Not All RAGs Are Created Equal: A Component-Wise Empirical Study for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14503)
*Qiang Ke,Yanjie Zhao,Hongjin Leng,Shengming Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对RAG流水线中的21+模型和方法进行了逐组件的实证研究，发现检索器组件（尤其是BM25）对软件工程任务性能的影响通常大于生成器模型的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG在软件工程中被越来越多地用于将LLMs与软件制品结合，但其组件的最优配置仍缺乏系统性的指导，导致实践中依赖昂贵且随机的试错。

Method: 对RAG流水线进行逐组件分解，系统评估了4种查询处理技术、7种检索模型（稀疏/稠密/混合）、4种上下文精炼方法和6种生成器，在代码生成、摘要和修复3个核心SE任务上进行测试。

Result: 检索器组件（尤其是检索算法的选择）对系统性能的影响通常大于生成器模型；经典的词法检索器BM25在不同任务中表现出异常稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 为构建面向软件工程的有效RAG系统提供了数据驱动的实践指南，明确了优化工作的优先顺序。

Abstract: While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly adopted to ground Large Language Models (LLMs) in software artifacts, the optimal configuration of its components remains an open question for software engineering (SE) tasks. The lack of systematic guidance forces practitioners into costly, ad-hoc experimentation. This paper presents a comprehensive, component-wise empirical study that dissects the RAG pipeline, evaluating over 21 distinct models and methods. Our study systematically isolates and evaluates 4 query processing techniques, 7 retrieval models spanning sparse, dense, and hybrid paradigms, 4 context refinement methods, and 6 distinct generators. We test these components on a suite of 3 core SE tasks: code generation, summarization, and repair. Our empirical findings reveal a crucial insight: the retriever-side components, particularly the choice of the retrieval algorithm, often exert a more significant influence on final system performance than the selection of the generator model. Strikingly, the classic lexical retriever BM25 demonstrates exceptionally robust performance across diverse tasks. Our analysis provides a practical, data-driven roadmap for researchers and practitioners, offering clear guidance on prioritizing optimization efforts when constructing effective RAG systems for software engineering contexts.

</details>


### [176] [Viverra: Text-to-Code with Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14972)
*Haoze Wu,Rocky Klopfenstein,Keith Farkas,Nina Narodytska*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Viverra是一个能自动为LLM生成的C代码附带形式化验证注释的系统，它通过组合式有界模型检查器验证断言，帮助开发者理解代码并提高代码理解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-Code（文本生成代码）存在根本性局限：无法保证生成代码的正确性，开发者仍需人工审查、测试和维护，而阅读LLM生成的低质量代码耗时费力，可能抵消AI编程工具带来的效率提升。

Method: Viverra首先利用LLM根据自然语言任务描述合成C程序及候选断言（表达安全性和正确性属性），然后通过组合式有界模型检查器（bounded model checkers）以组合式（compositional）和尽力而为（best-effort）的方式验证这些断言。

Result: 在18个多样化编程任务上的评估表明，Viverra能够高效生成带有已验证断言的代码；包含400多名参与者的用户研究进一步证实，这些断言显著提升了用户在代码理解任务上的表现。

Conclusion: Viverra通过自动生成并验证形式化断言，有效弥补了Text-to-Code缺乏正确性保证的短板，在提升代码可理解性和开发者效率方面具有实际价值。

Abstract: A fundamental limitation of Text-to-Code is that no guarantee can be obtained about the correctness of the generated code. Therefore, to ensure its correctness, the generated code still has to be reviewed, tested, and maintained by developers. However, parsing through LLM-generated code can be tedious and time-consuming, potentially negating the productivity gains promised by AI-coding tools. To address this challenge, we present Viverra, a system that automatically produces formally verified annotations alongside generated code to aid user's understanding of the generated program. Given a natural-language task description, Viverra prompts an LLM to synthesize a C program together with candidate assertions expressing safety and correctness properties. It then verifies those assertions in a compositional and best-effort manner via a portfolio of bounded model checkers. Evaluation on 18 diverse programming tasks suggests that Viverra can efficiently generate code with verified assertions, and that these assertions improve users' performance on code-comprehension tasks in a user study with more than 400 participants.

</details>


### [177] [Mining Subscenario Refactoring Opportunities in Behaviour-Driven Software Test Suites: ML Classifiers and LLM-Judge Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14568)
*Ali Hassaan Mughal,Noor Fatima,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化方法，用于在BDD测试套件中挖掘值得重构的重复步骤子序列（切片），并将其映射到三种已知的重构模式。通过对339个Gherkin仓库的分析，训练了一个XGBoost分类器（F1=0.891），发现75.0%、59.5%和11.7%的场景分别包含三种重构候选模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD测试套件中积累了重复的步骤子序列，虽然有三种已发布的重构模式，但缺乏自动化方法来判断哪些重复子序列值得提取以及应该使用哪种机制。

Method: 使用滑动窗口（L∈[2,18]）从339个仓库的Gherkin语料中提取连续步骤切片，通过SBERT+UMAP+HDBSCAN进行 paraphrase-robust 聚类。三位作者对200个分层抽样切片进行标注，训练XGBoost分类器（5折交叉验证），并与规则基线和两种开源LLM裁判进行比较。

Result: 矿工产生了5,382,249个切片，合并为692,020个重复模式。三位作者的Fleiss' kappa=0.56（提取价值）和0.79（机制）。XGBoost分类器F1=0.891（95% CI [0.852, 0.927]），优于规则基线（F1=0.836, p=0.017）和最佳LLM裁判（F1=0.728, p<1e-4）。75.0%、59.5%和11.7%的场景分别包含三种重构候选。

Conclusion: 基于 paraphrase-robust 的子场景发现方法能够提供BDD重构机会的全语料普查；管道、分类器预测、标注池和评分标准均以Apache-2.0许可开源。

Abstract: Context. Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) software test suites accumulate duplicated step subsequences. Three published refactoring patterns are available (within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario invocation, cross-organisational shared higher-level step), but no prior work automates which recurring subsequences are worth extracting or which mechanism applies. Objective. Rank recurring step subsequences ("slices") by refactoring suitability (extraction-worthy), pre-map each to one of the three patterns, and quantify prevalence across the public BDD ecosystem. Method. Every contiguous L-step window (L in [2, 18]) in a 339-repository / 276-upstream-owner Gherkin corpus is keyed by paraphrase-robust cluster identifiers and counted under three scopes. Sentence-BERT (SBERT) / Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) / Hierarchical Density-Based Clustering (HDBSCAN) recovers paraphrase-equivalent slices. Three authors label a stratified 200-slice pool against a written rubric. An eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) extraction-worthy classifier trained under 5-fold cross-validation is compared with a tuned rule baseline and two open-weight Large Language Model (LLM) judges. Results. The miner produces 5,382,249 slices collapsing to 692,020 recurring patterns. Three-author Fleiss' kappa = 0.56 (extraction-worthy) and 0.79 (mechanism). The classifier reaches out-of-fold F1 = 0.891 (95% CI [0.852, 0.927]), outperforming both the rule baseline (F1 = 0.836, p = 0.017) and the better LLM judge (F1 = 0.728, p < 1e-4). 75.0%, 59.5%, and 11.7% of scenarios carry a within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario, or cross-organisational shared-step candidate. Conclusion. Paraphrase-robust subscenario discovery yields a corpus-wide census of BDD refactoring opportunities; pipeline, classifier predictions, labelled pool, and rubric are released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [178] [In-IDE Toolkit for Developers of AI-Based Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14612)
*Yaroslav Sokolov,Yury Khudyakov,Lenar Sharipov,Andrei Gasparian,Parth Tiwary,Artem Trofimov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个JetBrains IDE插件——AI Toolkit，它将LLM和智能体工作流的追踪与评估功能直接集成到IDE的运行/调试循环中，通过IDE原生工作流（运行触发追踪、分层检查、一键添加数据集、单元测试式评估）降低非ML背景开发者的AI开发门槛，初步使用数据显示了良好的采用率和低流失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM和智能体工作流的AI功能难以测试、调试和复现，尤其对于没有机器学习背景的软件工程师而言挑战巨大，现有工具缺乏与日常IDE工作流的深度集成。

Method: 通过混合方法研究提炼出三项核心需求（评估可重复、执行时刻暴露追踪、最小化设置和上下文切换），设计并实现了IDE原生工作流：运行触发追踪捕获、即时分层检查、一键"添加到数据集"、基于插件化指标的单元测试式评估，并在PyCharm中发布首个版本。

Result: 初步遥测数据显示：运行时提示时有较强的转化率，捕获追踪的用户持续使用率高，用户流失率低，表明IDE原生可观测性降低了激活能量并帮助开发者采用规范化实践。

Conclusion: 将AI可观测性和评估集成到日常IDE工作流中，能够使非ML专家的开发者也能采用现代AI开发实践，同时保持软件工程规范。

Abstract: AI-enabled features built on LLMs and agentic workflows are difficult to test, debug, and reproduce, especially for product-focused software engineers without a machine learning background. We present the AI Toolkit plugin for JetBrains IDEs, which brings tracing and evaluation directly into the Run/Debug loop. A mixed methods study with practitioners presents three consistent needs: (1) make evaluation regular and repeatable, (2) expose traces at the moment of execution, and (3) minimize setup and context switching. Guided by these needs, the AI Toolkit introduces an IDE-native workflow: run-triggered trace capture; immediate, hierarchical inspection; one-click "Add to Dataset" from traces; and unit-test-like evaluations with pluggable metrics. The first release in PyCharm shows promising early signals - strong conversion when promoted at Run, sustained usage among those who capture traces, and low churn - suggesting that IDE-native observability lowers activation energy and helps developers adopt disciplined practices. We detail the design and implementation of the AI Agents Debugger and AI Evaluation, report initial adoption telemetry, and outline next steps to broaden framework coverage and scale evaluations. Together, these results indicate that integrating AI observability and evaluation into everyday IDE workflows can make modern AI development accessible to non-ML specialists while preserving software-engineering practices.

</details>


### [179] [Documentation-Guided Agentic Codebase Migration from C to Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14634)
*Minh Le-Anh,Anh Nguyen Hoang,Bach Le,Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RustPrint是一个文档引导的智能体框架，用于仓库级别的C到Rust代码迁移。它首先生成架构感知文档作为蓝图，让编码智能体据此规划crate、实现模块、检查编译、减少unsafe代码，并通过文档对比和测试运行进行修复。在8个真实C仓库（11K-84K行代码）上，RustPrint在开源和闭源骨干模型下均能完全编译所有目标，特征保留率达93.26%，交叉评估测试通过率达95.17%，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将遗留C仓库迁移到Rust可以提升内存安全性，但现有翻译工具仅工作在文件或函数级别，丢失了架构意图，导致仓库级别的迁移不可靠。

Method: RustPrint首先将源码转换为架构感知文档作为迁移蓝图，包含模块结构、数据流、API和设计原理；然后由编码智能体基于蓝图规划crate、实现模块、检查编译性、减少unsafe代码并迭代优化；接着将Rust输出文档与原文档对比，将不匹配作为修复信号；同时翻译并运行源测试套件，利用运行时失败指导定向修复。

Result: 在8个11K-84K LoC的真实C仓库上，RustPrint在开源（Kimi-K2-Instruct）和闭源（GPT-5.4）骨干下均能完全编译所有目标。使用Kimi-K2-Instruct时，特征保留率达93.26%（对比Claude Code基线的52.52%），交叉评估测试通过率达95.17%（对比79.85%）。

Conclusion: 文档引导的协调机制是实现可扩展代码库迁移的有效方向。

Abstract: Migrating legacy C repositories to Rust promises stronger memory safety, but existing translators often work at the level of files or functions and miss architectural intent. We present RustPrint, a documentation-guided agentic framework for repository-level C-to-Rust migration. RustPrint first converts the source repository into architecture-aware documentation and treats it as a migration blueprint capturing module structure, data flow, APIs, and design rationale. Coding agents then use this blueprint to plan crates, implement modules, check compilability, reduce unsafe code, and iteratively refine the translated repository. RustPrint next compares documentation from the Rust output against the source documentation and uses mismatches as repair signals. It also translates and runs source test suites so runtime failures can guide targeted fixes. Experiments on eight real-world C repositories ranging from 11K to 84K LoC show that RustPrint compiles every target under both an open-weight (Kimi-K2-Instruct) and a closed-weight (GPT-5.4) backbone, while prior LLM-based translators (Self-Repair, EvoC2Rust) fail repository-wide. With the open-weight Kimi-K2-Instruct backbone, RustPrint exceeds an agentic Claude Code baseline on feature preservation (93.26% vs. 52.52%) and on cross-evaluation test pass rate (95.17% vs. 79.85%). These results suggest that documentation-guided coordination is a useful direction for scalable codebase migration.

</details>


### [180] [Agentic AI in Industry: Adoption Level and Deployment Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14675)
*Spyridon Alvanakis Apostolou,Jan Bosch,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对16名来自12家不同规模企业的从业者访谈，揭示了当前工业界在采纳Agentic AI时存在一个关键的"能力-部署验证鸿沟"：四家公司拥有高级实验性AI能力，但因缺乏充分的输出验证机制而无法将其整合到生产流程中，仅有"人在回路"被视为可信赖的验证手段。研究采用六级成熟度框架，发现7家公司处于Level 1（AI助手）、4家处于Level 2（AI补偿器）、仅1家达到Level 3（多智能体编排），大型安全敏感企业是较先进的采纳者。四大障碍包括：上下文窗口限制、专有编程语言性能不足、非确定性与资格认证标准不兼容、数据保密性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Agentic AI系统正进入软件工程工作流，但关于工业企业如何实际采纳它们的实证证据仍然稀少，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用定性访谈研究方法，对16名来自12家不同规模和领域公司的从业者进行半结构化访谈，并采用六级成熟度框架（改编自已建立的AI驱动组织）对企业的采纳状态进行分类和分析。

Result: 发现了核心的"能力-部署验证鸿沟"：四家公司具备高级实验性AI能力但因缺乏输出验证机制无法投入生产；识别出四大障碍（上下文窗口限制、专有语言性能不足、非确定性与认证标准冲突、数据保密性）；揭示了该鸿沟的两个相互依赖的维度（信息不对称和资格认证缺失）。

Conclusion: 人与验证机制是目前工业级Agentic AI部署的核心瓶颈，提出了信息不对称和资格认证缺失两个维度共同构成了工业agentic集成的核心开放问题。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are entering software engineering workflows, yet empirical evidence on how industrial organizations actually adopt them remains sparse. We present a qualitative interview study with sixteen practitioners across twelve companies of varying size and domain. This study characterizes the current agentic AI adoption state of these companies, employing a six-level maturity framework adapted from established AI-driven organizations. The findings reveal that seven companies operate at Level~1 (AI Assistants), four companies at Level~2 (AI Compensators), and only one in Level~3 (Multi-Agent Orchestration), with large and safety-regulated organizations among the most advanced adopters. The primary finding is a capability-deployment verification gap, four companies demonstrated higher-level experimental AI capabilities but cannot integrate them into production workflows because adequate output verification mechanisms are absent, leaving human-in-the-loop as the only trusted verification mechanism. This gap is shaped by four recurring barriers: context window of LLMs constraints especially when diverse knowledge aggregation is needed, under-performance on proprietary programming languages and protocols, non-determinism incompatible with qualification standards, and data confidentiality concerns. Two interdependent dimensions of this gap emerge from these findings (information asymmetry and qualification absence) framing a core open problem for industrial agentic integration.

</details>


### [181] [Towards In-Depth Root Cause Localization for Microservices with Multi-Agent Recursion-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14866)
*Lingzhe Zhang,Tong Jia,Kangjin Wang,Chiming Duan,Minghua He,Rongqian Wang,Xi Peng,Meiling Wang,Gong Zhang,Renhai Chen,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCLAgent，一种基于多智能体递归思维链与并行推理的微服务系统根因定位框架，通过将诊断过程沿调用图分解、为每个span分配专用智能体并递归并行组织，解决了现有LLM方法中上下文爆炸和串行推理两大问题，在多个公开基准上实现了更优的定位精度和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务系统因动态交互和运行时环境演化而日益复杂，故障频发。现有方法中，传统机器学习和深度学习方法可解释性差且跨部署迁移能力弱，而基于大语言模型的方法存在上下文爆炸（稀释关键证据、降低定位精度）和串行推理结构（限制因果探索深度、损害推理效率）两大根本性局限。通过研究人类SRE实际诊断过程及现有LLM方法的不足，作者提出了更优的根因定位框架。

Method: RCLAgent框架沿调用图对诊断过程进行分解，为每个span分配一个专用智能体（Dedicated Agent），并依据图拓扑结构以递归和并行方式组织这些智能体。最终通过合成根级诊断报告（Root-Level Diagnosis Report）和全局证据图（Global Evidence Graph）得出最终诊断结论，实现了多智能体递归思维（Recursion-of-Thought）与并行推理。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，RCLAgent在定位精度和推理效率两方面均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCLAgent通过模仿人类SRE的诊断实践，利用多智能体递归并行推理范式，有效解决了LLM方法在微服务根因定位中的上下文爆炸和串行推理瓶颈，为提升系统可靠性提供了一种高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: As modern microservice systems grow increasingly complex due to dynamic interactions and evolving runtime environments, they experience failures with rising frequency. Ensuring system reliability therefore critically depends on accurate root cause localization (RCL). While numerous traditional machine learning and deep learning approaches have been explored for this task, they often suffer from limited interpretability and poor transferability across deployments. More recently, large language model (LLM)-based methods have been proposed to address these issues. However, existing LLM-based approaches still face two fundamental limitations: context explosion, which dilutes critical evidence and degrades localization accuracy, and serial reasoning structures, which hinder deep causal exploration and impair inference efficiency. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive study of both how human SREs perform root cause localization in practice and why existing LLM-based methods fall short. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RCLAgent, an in-depth root cause localization framework for microservice systems that realizes multi-agent recursion-of-thought with parallel reasoning. RCLAgent decomposes the diagnostic process along the trace graph by assigning each span to a Dedicated Agent and organizing agents recursively and in parallel according to the graph topology, with the final diagnosis obtained by synthesizing the Root-Level Diagnosis Report and the Global Evidence Graph. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that RCLAgent consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both localization accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [182] [Veritas: A Semantically Grounded Agentic Framework for Memory Corruption Vulnerability Detection in Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15097)
*Xinran Zheng,Alfredo Pesoli,Marco Valleri,Suman Jana,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Veritas 是一个用于二进制内存损坏漏洞检测的语义化框架，结合静态切片器、双视角LLM检测器和多智能体验证器，在真实世界的二进制漏洞基准上实现了90%的召回率且无假阳性，并发现了一个此前未知的Apple漏洞（已分配CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去除符号信息的二进制程序中检测内存损坏漏洞需要从低层次、有损的表示中恢复对象语义、跨过程传播和可行触发条件。现有基于LLM的方法虽然提升了代码理解能力，但可靠的检测仍需建立在对内存相关语义和运行时可行性证据的扎实基础之上。

Method: Veritas 包含三个核心组件：1) 静态切片器，基于RetDec提升后的LLVM IR重建值流关系（def-use、调用、返回、全局变量和指针操作），生成紧凑的、带证据支持的流对象；2) 双视角LLM检测器，结合反编译C代码和选择性LLVM IR，基于已接地（grounded）的流对象逐步推理控制流、边界和对象对应关系；3) 多智能体验证器，通过引导式调试、断点检查和内存检查预言机来确认或拒绝候选漏洞。

Result: 在真实世界二进制漏洞基准上，Veritas 达到90%的召回率。在假阳性评估中，对623个检测器候选进行穷举验证和人工审计，穷举子集无假阳性，额外审计确认2个假阳性。在真实应用中，发现了一个此前未知的Apple漏洞，已确认并分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: 结果表明，语义化接地（semantic grounding）可以作为实用的二进制漏洞检测的操作性设计原则，有效提升检测的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Detecting memory corruption vulnerabilities in stripped binaries requires recovering object semantics, interprocedural propagation, and feasible triggers from low-level, lossy representations. Recent LLM-based approaches improve code understanding, but reliable detection still requires grounding in memory-relevant semantics and runtime feasibility evidence. We present Veritas, a semantically grounded framework for binary memory corruption vulnerability detection. Veritas combines a static slicer over RetDec-lifted LLVM IR, a dual-view LLM detector that reasons step by step over grounded flows using decompiled C and selective LLVM IR, and a multi-agent validator that checks hypotheses against debugger-visible artifacts and runtime evidence. The slicer reconstructs value-flow relations from LLVM-IR facts, including def-use, calls, returns, globals, and pointer operations, and emits compact witness-backed flow objects. The detector uses these artifacts to reason about control flow, bounds, and object correspondence without rediscovering whole-binary propagation. The validator confirms or rejects candidates through guided debugging, breakpoint inspection, and memory-checking oracles. We implement Veritas as a modular pipeline and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of real-world binary vulnerability cases. Veritas achieves 90\% recall. For false-positive assessment, we exhaustively validate and manually verify 623 detector candidates and audit additional candidates from larger cases. The exhaustive subset produces no false positives, while the additional audit identifies two confirmed false positives. In a real-world application, Veritas discovered a previously unknown Apple vulnerability that was confirmed and assigned a CVE. These results support semantic grounding as an operational design principle for practical binary vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [183] [CA2: Code-Aware Agent for Automated Game Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13918)
*Valliappan Chidambaram Adaikkappan,Vincent Martineau,Joshua Romoff,David Meger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Code Aware Agent (CA2)，利用调用栈信息增强强化学习游戏测试，相比不利用代码信号的基线方法在状态和图像两种环境中均取得一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化游戏测试成本高、耗时长，手动测试易遗漏边缘情况，现有自动化方法难以实现完整代码覆盖率，且先前基于强化学习的游戏测试工作未利用调用栈等内部代码信号。

Method: 设计CA2智能体，接收当前函数调用轨迹和游戏状态，学习到达特定目标函数的测试策略；在基于状态和基于图像的两种环境中实现高效的调用栈提取。

Result: CA2在两种环境（State-based和Image-based）中均比不利用调用栈信息的基线方法取得一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 融入调用栈等代码信号能够使游戏测试更加有效和具有目标性。

Abstract: Automated game testing is important for verifying game functionality, but it remains a costly and time-consuming process. Manual testing often misses edge cases, and current automated methods struggle to provide full code coverage. Prior work has explored reinforcement learning (RL) for game testing, but without leveraging internal code signals such as the call stack. We present Code Aware Agent (CA2), which uses call stack information to learn effective testing strategies. The agent receives the current function call trace along with the game state and learns to reach specific target functions. We instrument two types of environments, 1) State-based and 2) Image-based, with support for efficient call stack extraction. Through experimental evaluation, we find that CA2 achieves consistent improvement over the non-code aware baselines, which does not leverage call stack information. Our results show that incorporating code signals like the call stack enables more effective and targeted game testing.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Self-Regulated Learning in Essay Writing: Consistency of Strategies and Impact on Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14228)
*Gloria Fernández-Nieto,Kiyoshige Garcés,Mladen Raković,Tongguang Li,Xinyu Li,Linxuan Zhao,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过过程挖掘和机器学习分析93名（第一轮）和95名（第二轮）哥伦比亚中学生在数字平台上完成在线论文写作任务时的元认知轨迹数据，识别出三种主导的SRL策略，发现“先密集写作、后选择性阅读”策略虽不常见但与学习结果正相关，而多数学生采用或转向“先阅读、后写作”策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青春期是自我调节学习能力巩固的关键期，在线和混合式教育的普及使SRL能力愈发重要，但目前对中学生在在线环境中如何自我调节、其SRL过程与策略如何随时间演变及如何影响学习结果知之甚少，重复测量设计的证据匮乏。

Method: 采用过程挖掘与无监督机器学习相结合的方法，分析从数字学习平台收集的中学生两轮在线论文写作任务（间隔一周）中的元认知相关轨迹数据。

Result: 识别出三种主导SRL策略；多数学生保持或转向“先阅读、后写作”策略；第二轮中无学生使用“先密集写作、后选择性阅读”策略；后者虽不常见但与学习结果正相关。

Conclusion: 中学生在在线学习环境中的SRL策略具有可变性，特定策略（先密集写作后选择性阅读）虽使用率低但预示更好的学习结果，为设计在线支持机制提供了依据。

Abstract: Background: Abilities for effective self-regulated learning (SRL) are critical for lifelong learning, particularly during adolescence when these skills consolidate and strongly influence future learning. Their importance has grown with the rise of online and blended education. Yet, little is known about how secondary school students self-regulate in online environments, how their SRL processes and strategies evolve, or how they affect outcomes. In secondary education, understanding these processes can reveal patterns and indicators of learning success, informing the design of online support mechanisms. Evidence from repeated-measures designs remains scarce. Objectives: This study aims to examine how secondary school students enact SRL strategies during online essay writing, how these strategies change over time, and how they relate to learning outcomes. Methods: We analysed metacognition-related trace data collected from secondary students during a two-wave online essay-writing task conducted one week apart in two Colombian schools (N = 93 for session 1, N = 95 for session 2) via a digital learning platform. Using a combination of process mining and unsupervised machine learning techniques, we identified dominant SRL strategies grounded in established SRL processes and examined their stability and association with learning outcomes. Results and conclusions: Three dominant SRL strategies were identified. Results showed variability: many students remained in or shifted to Read first, write next, while none used Write intensively, read selectively in session 2. Although less common, latter strategy was positively associated with learning outcomes.

</details>


### [185] [A Formative Study of Brief Affective Text as a Complement to Wearable Sensing for Longitudinal Student Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14360)
*Tamunotonye Harry,Johanna Hidalgo,Matthew Price,Yuanyuan Feng,Kathryn Stanton,Connie Tompkins,Peter Sheridan Dodds,Mikaela Irene Fudolig,Laura Bloomfield,Christopher Danforth*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对458名大学生（3,610人-波次）为期一年的追踪，结合Oura戒指被动传感数据和双月开放式简短文本书写（中位数3词），发现利用NLP方法从超简短文本中提取的情感维度（而非主题内容）能够显著增强被动生理数据的心理可解释性，其中通用预训练嵌入模型效果最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备能高保真捕获生理和行为数据，但仅靠传感器数据难以恢复塑造这些结果的心理背景，限制了被动感知在数字健康中的应用。研究者希望探索超简短自然语言文本能否作为被动感知的可扩展补充，以低成本增强数据的心理可解释性。

Method: 458名大学生佩戴Oura戒指完成一年追踪（每两月一次开放式询问"最担忧什么"），共3,610人-波次数据。采用基于词典、通用预训练、领域微调三种NLP方法，通过被试内混合效应模型分析文本特征与九项睡眠及体力活动指标的关联。

Result: 学术担忧主导的周次与体力活动降低相关，情绪耗竭语言主导的周次与睡眠质量下降和心率变异性降低相关。通用预训练嵌入优于领域微调模型；零样本主题分类无显著关联，而三种方法的情感维度均持续呈现显著关联，表明情感基调而非主题内容携带核心信号。

Conclusion: 超简短情感开放式提示能以极低负担丰富被动生理数据的心理可解释性，为数字健康中的被动-主动数据融合提供设计指导。

Abstract: Wearable devices capture physiological and behavioral data with increasing fidelity, but the psychological context shaping these outcomes is difficult to recover from sensor data alone, limiting passive sensing utility for digital health. We examined whether ultra-brief naturalistic concern text could serve as a scalable complement to passive sensing. In a year-long study of 458 university students (3,610 person-waves) tracked with Oura rings, participants responded bimonthly to an open-ended prompt about what concerned them most; responses had a median length of three words. We compared dictionary-based, general pretrained, and domain-adapted NLP approaches using within-person mixed-effects models across nine sleep and physical activity outcomes. Weeks dominated by academic concern framing were associated with lower physical activity; weeks characterized by emotional exhaustion language were associated with poorer sleep quality and lower heart rate variability. General pretrained embeddings outperformed domain-adapted models for most outcomes, with domain adaptation showing relative advantage for autonomic outcomes. Zero-shot classification of concern topics produced no significant associations, while affective dimensions across all three methods were consistently associated with outcomes, indicating emotional register rather than topical content carries the signal. These findings offer design guidance: ultra-brief affective prompts enrich the psychological interpretability of passive physiological data at minimal burden.

</details>


### [186] [SmartWalkCoach: An AI Companion for End-to-End Walking Guidance, Motivation, and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14628)
*Xianzhe Zhang,Mingxuan Hu,Bufan Xue,Erick Purwanto,Thomas J Selig,Daniel Yonto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SmartWalkCoach是一个移动AI伴侣系统，通过三个轻量化智能体（GeographyAgent、AccompanyAgent、SummaryAgent）支持步行全程（规划、引导、反思）。12人交叉实验表明，在信息提示基础上增加动机性对话可显著提升积极情绪和用户体验，并揭示了移动伴侣的两个设计要点：支持性关系表达和上下文感知时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补地图导航与动机性教练之间的空白，现有系统缺乏对步行全程的认知负荷降低和持续参与激励的支持。

Method: 提出三智能体架构：GeographyAgent（会话式路线策划+调用地图API）、AccompanyAgent（上下文感知的及时提示，融合信息与鼓励）、SummaryAgent（简洁反思与下一步规划）。采用两周期AB/BA交叉设计（N=12），对比信息仅提供 vs 信息+动机两种条件。

Result: 线性混合模型显示，增加动机性对话显著提升积极情绪和用户体验，无携带效应。主题分析揭示两个设计要点：支持性关系表达和上下文感知时机（如避开高负荷时刻、在疲劳/里程碑时干预）。

Conclusion: 端到端工具型智能体架构可降低步行规划与陪伴中的认知负荷，受控现场实验证实上下文感知动机对情感和用户体验的增益，为mHealth伴侣提供了表达、时机和频率的设计指导。

Abstract: We present SmartWalkCoach, a mobile AI companion that supports the full walking journey: from pre-walk planning to in-walk guidance through to post-walk reflection. Addressing a gap between map navigation and motivational coaching, SmartWalkCoach orchestrates three lightweight agents: (1) GeographyAgent for conversational route curation from nearby points of interest and user preferences while delegating pathfinding to map APIs; (2) AccompanyAgent for context-aware, just-in-time prompts that blend informational cues with relational encouragement; and (3) SummaryAgent for concise reflection and next-step planning. This end-to-end, tool-using design aims to lower cognitive load in planning and sustain engagement and motivation during walking through delivering dynamic, cadence-aware interventions. We conducted an in-the-wild, two-period AB/BA crossover study (N=12), where each participant completed two comparable walks with counterbalanced conditions: Information-only versus Information+Motivation. Linear mixed models show that adding motivational, companion-like dialogue significantly improved outcomes: participants reported higher positive feelings and better user experience, with no evidence of carryover. Thematic analysis surfaced two design imperatives for mobile companions: supportive, relational expression and context-aware timing (e.g., avoiding high-load moments, intervening at fatigue/milestones). Our contributions are: (i) an end-to-end, tool-using agent architecture for everyday walking that reduces cognitive load during planning and accompaniment; (ii) a controlled field evaluation linking context-aware motivation to affect and UX gains; and (iii) actionable design guidance on expression, timing, and frequency for mHealth companions.We outline limitations and paths toward multimodal, voice-first companions, with adaptive personalization mechanisms.

</details>


### [187] [Beliefs and Misconceptions around Integrated Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14849)
*William Seymour,Adam Jenkins,Mark Cote,Jose Such*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索了用户如何理解和使用内置于浏览器中的对话式AI（Copilot），发现用户依赖对LLM和互联网搜索的既有认知来形成提示策略，且引用的存在提升了答案可信度，但用户往往不会核实这些引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的对话AI逐渐嵌入现有工作流程，其来源和训练痕迹被淡化，用户对其的认知和信任机制尚不明确，需要深入探究用户如何理解、使用并判断这类AI输出的可信度。

Method: 进行了一项用户研究，20名参与者在Microsoft Edge中使用Copilot AI功能完成信息检索和规划任务，通过观察和分析用户的提示策略、信任判断及事实核查行为来收集数据。

Result: 参与者依赖既有认知（对LLM和互联网搜索的理解）来形成提示策略；引用增加了回答的可信度，但参与者很少主动核实引用；参与者事实核查时往往倾向于查找与CAI相同的信息来源。

Conclusion: 用户对嵌入浏览器的对话AI的信任受引用呈现影响，但存在“信任但未验证”的风险，且用户的信息核查行为具有来源偏向性。

Abstract: LLM-driven conversational AI is beginning to disappear into the background, shifting from something used directly towards something increasingly integrated into existing workflows. In the process, markers of origin and training are smoothed away as LLMs become commodified in the eyes of users. We explore how people approach using a web browser with conversational AI built in, focusing on how they develop their understanding and determine whether to trust its outputs. We conducted a study where 20 participants used the Copilot AI features in Microsoft Edge to conduct information retrieval and planning tasks. Participants relied on a combination of existing perceptions of LLMs and internet search, tracing the effect of beliefs about how Copilot generated answers on prompting strategies. The inclusion of citations increased the trustworthiness of answers without participants feeling the need to be check them, with participants often reaching for the same information sources as the CAI when fact-checking.

</details>


### [188] [Towards Gaze-Informed AI Disclosure Interfaces: Eye-Tracking Attentional and Cognitive Load While Reading AI-Assisted News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14999)
*Pooja Prajod,Hannes Cools,Thomas Röggla,Pablo Cesar,Abdallah El Ali*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过3×2×2混合因素实验设计，研究了AI使用披露的详细程度对读者注意力和认知负荷的影响。研究发现，简短的一句话披露会导致更高的注视时长和眼跳次数，即产生注意力成本，而详细披露不会增加额外负担。基于信息鸿沟理论，简短的标签可能触发视觉审查。NASA-TLX和瞳孔直径无显著差异，表明AI披露不增加认知负荷。受访者偏好详细或"按需详细"的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注AI披露对信任和可信度的影响，但披露对读者注意力和认知负荷的影响尚未得到充分探索。随着生成式AI在新闻业中的普及，如何设计既能告知读者又不增加不必要负担的AI使用披露成为关键挑战。

Method: 采用3×2×2混合因素实验设计，操作变量包括：AI使用披露详细程度（无披露、一句话披露、详细披露）、新闻类型（政治、生活）、AI角色（编辑、部分内容生成）。通过NASA-TXL量表和眼动追踪技术测量认知负荷和注意力。

Result: 一句话披露导致显著更高的注视时长和眼跳次数，尤其对于AI编辑的内容；详细披露未增加额外负担。NASA-TLX得分和瞳孔直径在各条件下无显著差异。访谈显示受访者偏好详细或"按需详细"的披露设计。

Conclusion: 简短AI披露会触发读者的视觉审查行为，产生注意力成本；详细披露不会增加认知负担。建议设计基于注视行为的自适应披露界面，根据读者注意力模式和新闻上下文动态调整透明度水平。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly integrated into journalism, designing effective AI-use disclosures that inform readers without imposing unnecessary burden is a key challenge. While prior research has primarily focused on trust and credibility, the impact of disclosures on readers' attentional and cognitive load remains underexplored. To address this gap, we conducted a $3\times2\times2$ mixed factorial study manipulating the level of AI-use disclosure detail (none, one-line, detailed), news type (politics, lifestyle), and role of AI (editing, partial content generation), measuring load via NASA-TLX and eye-tracking. Our results reveal a significant attentional cost: one-line disclosures resulted in significantly higher fixation durations and saccade counts, particularly for AI-edited content. Detailed disclosures did not impose additional burden. Drawing on Information-Gap Theory, we argue that brief labels may trigger increased visual scrutiny by alerting readers to AI use without providing enough information. NASA-TLX scores and pupil diameter showed no significant differences across conditions, suggesting that AI-use disclosures do not impose cognitive burden regardless of the detail level. Interview insights contextualize these findings and reveal a strong preference for detailed or ``detail-on-demand'' designs. Our findings inform the design of gaze-informed adaptive disclosure interfaces that dynamically adjust transparency levels based on readers' attentional patterns and news context.

</details>


### [189] [Deceptive Cookies: Consent by Design -- A Mixed Methods Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15056)
*Liv Hilde Sjøflot,Tobias A. Opsahl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过可用性测试和调查（N=20）发现，用户虽然倾向于拒绝Cookie收集，但由于Cookie同意横幅中存在欺骗性设计模式，导致用户最终往往接受。用户更愿意信任的网站或能改善体验时同意。撤回同意平均耗时是给予同意的20倍以上，表明当前Cookie同意横幅在用户自主权方面不理想，导致用户"设计性同意"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解用户在管理Cookie同意时，其陈述的隐私偏好与实际行为之间存在差距的影响因素。

Method: 采用混合方法，通过可用性测试和问卷调查收集数据（N=20）。

Result: 1) 用户虽想拒绝Cookie收集，但因欺骗性设计模式而接受；2) 用户对信任的网站或预期能改善体验时更愿意同意；3) 撤回同意平均耗时比给予同意长20倍以上。

Conclusion: 当前Cookie同意横幅在用户自主权方面存在不足，导致用户"设计性同意"，即被设计诱导而非自主选择同意。

Abstract: While companies increasingly rely on data, especially when it comes to targeted advertising, adapting content to users, selling data and training machine learning models, the collection of data raises privacy concerns. One way of collecting data is by using HTTP cookies when interacting with a website. Legal regulations require service providers to collect consent for some forms of cookie collection, which is often acquired through \emph{cookie consent banners}, but their effectiveness has been debated. This study aims to understand what influences users' experience and behaviour when managing their cookie consent, by investigating the gap between their stated privacy preferences and their actual actions. A mixed methods approach was used, collecting data from a usability test and a survey (N=20). The results showed that although participants generally want to reject cookie collection, they often end up accepting because of deceptive patterns in the cookie consent banner design. It also showed that they were more willing to consent to websites they trusted and if they expected it would improve their user experience. Although the current EU legislation states that withdrawing consent must be as easy as giving it, withdrawing consent took on average more than 20 times longer than giving it. This suggests that cookie consent banners in their current form are not ideal with respect to user autonomy, often leading users to \emph{consent by design}.

</details>


### [190] [Usable but Conventional: An Empirical Study on the UX of AI-Generated Interface Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15124)
*Karoline Romero,Igor Wiese,Renato Balancieiri,Gislaine Camila Leal,Guilherme Guerino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过92名参与者的实证调查，评估了生成式AI工具制作的原型与人类制作的原型在用户体验上的差异。研究发现AI生成的界面在实用维度（可用性、效率）获得积极评价，但在享乐维度（原创性、创新性）评价中性或负面，表明GenAI能产生功能性界面但倾向于强化视觉和结构模式，影响原创性感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式AI工具制作的原型在用户体验上的表现，特别是与人类制作原型的差异，以及AI生成内容在实用性和原创性方面的表现。

Method: 采用实证调查研究方法，招募92名参与者，使用UEQ-S量表评估AI生成和人类制作的原型，评估前不告知参与者原型作者身份，测量实用维度和享乐维度的用户体验。

Result: AI生成的原型在实用维度（可用性、效率）上获得积极评价，但在享乐维度（原创性、创新性）上评价为中性或负面。

Conclusion: 生成式AI能够产生功能性良好的界面原型，但由于其倾向于强化已有的视觉和结构模式，导致在原创性感知方面表现不足。

Abstract: This paper investigates User Experience (UX) with prototypes generated by Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools. An empirical survey with 92 participants evaluated AI-generated and human-created prototypes without prior identification of authorship. We measured UX using the UEQ-S, covering pragmatic and hedonic dimensions. Results indicate positive evaluations in pragmatic aspects, such as usability and efficiency, and neutral or negative evaluations in hedonic aspects, including originality and innovation. We concluded that GenAI can produce functional interfaces but tends to reinforce visual and structural patterns that affect perceptions of originality.

</details>
