# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Proof Nets for PiL (Full Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14476)
*Matteo Acclavio,Giulia Manara*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文为PiL（π-演算的一阶乘法加法线性逻辑扩展）引入了证明网，提供了正确性准则、顺序化程序和证明翻译算法，并证明证明网能够提供规则置换下序贯演算推导的规范表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是将π-演算中的进程浅编码为公式，并利用线性逻辑的证明网理论为PiL提供一种规范化的、不依赖于规则置换的推导表示方法。

Method: 论文通过扩展一阶乘法加法线性逻辑（MALL）引入新算子来定义PiL，然后为其设计证明网结构，提出正确性准则（用于判断网结构是否对应合法证明），开发顺序化程序（将证明网还原为序贯演算推导），并实现证明翻译算法。

Result: 成功为PiL建立了完整的证明网理论，包括正确性准则、顺序化程序和证明翻译算法；证明了证明网能够提供序贯演算推导在规则置换下的规范（典范）表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为进程演算与线性逻辑之间的深层联系提供了新的理论工具，证明网的形式化为PiL中推导的等价性和规范化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce proof nets for PiL, an extension of first-order multiplicative additive linear logic with new operators allowing a shallow encoding of processes in the π-calculus as formulas. We provide correctness criterion, sequentialization procedure, and a proof translation algorithm. We show that proof nets provide a canonical representation of sequent calculus derivations modulo rule permutations.

</details>


### [192] [Extending CDCL to disjunctions of parity equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15002)
*Paul Beame,Glenn Sun*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出CDCL(⊕)，将CDCL从CNF扩展到XNF公式（包含奇偶性析取的约束），建立了与Res(⊕)证明系统的双向模拟关系，并通过原型求解器Xorcle在XNF基准测试上超越了Kissat和CryptoMiniSAT等现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDCL基于Resolution证明系统，对于Resolution难的问题难以处理。而更强的证明系统Res(⊕)（处理XNF公式，约束为线性子句）能产生指数级更短的证明。现有求解器如CryptoMiniSAT虽然能处理单独的奇偶性方程，但对更一般的线性子句推理探索不足。

Method: 1. 提出CDCL(⊕)框架，将CDCL推广到XNF公式，支持任意奇偶性方程的分支、单位传播中的线性代数推理、以及通过冲突分析学习线性子句。2. 建立与Res(⊕)的双向联系：CDCL(⊕)不仅能产生Res(⊕)证明，在非确定决策和重启下还能多项式模拟Res(⊕)。3. 引入新的Res(⊕)推理规则集，用于翻译1-UIP子句学习等基于Resolution的子程序。4. 实现原型求解器Xorcle，包含适配XNF的CDCL启发式策略和扩展的LRUP(⊕)证明日志系统。

Result: 在原生XNF公式基准测试上，Xorcle优于Kissat和CryptoMiniSAT。对于CNF形式的Tseitin公式，即使不进行预处理，Xorcle的运行时间也近乎多项式级缩放。

Conclusion: CDCL(⊕)成功将CDCL范式扩展到XNF公式，建立了与Res(⊕)证明系统的理论联系，并通过实验验证了其实际有效性，为奇偶性约束推理提供了新的高效求解框架。

Abstract: Because CDCL produces proofs in the Resolution proof system, problems provably hard for Resolution are also provably hard for CDCL. Exponentially shorter proofs can sometimes be found using stronger proof systems such as $\text{Res}(\oplus)$, a generalization of Resolution to XNF formulas, whose constraints are disjunctions of parity equations ("linear clauses") such as $(x \oplus y) \lor \lnot (y \oplus z)$. While some modern solvers like CryptoMiniSAT reason on Boolean clauses with separate parity equations, reasoning about more general linear clauses is less explored.
  We present $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$, a generalization of CDCL to XNF formulas, and prove a bidirectional connection with $\text{Res}(\oplus)$: $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$ not only produces $\text{Res}(\oplus)$ proofs, but also polynomially simulates $\text{Res}(\oplus)$ given nondeterministic decisions and restarts, mirroring the classical relationship between CDCL and Resolution. Our key technical tool is a new set of inference rules for $\text{Res}(\oplus)$ that helps us translate Resolution-based subroutines such as 1-UIP clause learning. Altogether, $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$'s parity reasoning includes branching on arbitrary parity equations, linear-algebraic reasoning during unit propagation, and learning linear clauses through conflict analysis.
  We provide a proof-of-concept implementation of $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$ called Xorcle, which includes adaptations of existing CDCL heuristics to XNF formulas and an extension of LRUP proof logging that we call $\text{LRUP}(\oplus)$. On a selected suite of benchmarks focusing on native XNF formulas, Xorcle outperforms existing solvers such as Kissat and CryptoMiniSAT. Additionally, on Tseitin formulas written in CNF, even without preprocessing, Xorcle's running time appears to scale nearly polynomially.

</details>


### [193] [The Guarded Fragment with Nested Equivalences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15072)
*Oskar Fiuk*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了带嵌套等价关系的守护片段（GF）的逻辑性质，证明在无等式情况下具有有限模型性质和可判定性，并给出了TOWER完全和(K+2)-ExpTime完全的复杂度边界，同时揭示了嵌套条件或等式约束的放宽会导致不可判定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GF是经典的可判定一阶逻辑片段，但引入等价关系后判定性面临挑战。本文旨在探索嵌套等价关系结构下GF的判定边界和计算复杂度。

Method: 定义一族嵌套等价关系（E1, E2, ...，其中E_{k+1}比E_k更粗），研究无等式GF在该语义下的模型性质和可判定性，通过复杂度归约建立上下界。

Result: 无等式GF + 嵌套等价关系具有有限模型性质和可判定性；一般情形TOWER-完全，固定K个关系时(K+2)-ExpTime-完全；去掉嵌套条件（即使只有两个等价关系）或引入等式（即使只有一个等价关系）均导致不可判定。

Conclusion: 嵌套等价关系在无等式GF中保持了可判定性，但该结果对条件放松高度敏感，精确刻画了可判定与不可判定的边界。

Abstract: The Guarded Fragment (GF) is a well-established decidable fragment of first-order logic. We study an extension of GF with nested equivalence relations, namely a family of distinguished binary predicates $E_1, E_2, \dots$ interpreted as equivalence relations such that $E_{k+1}$ is coarser than $E_k$ for every $k$. We show that the equality-free GF with nested equivalence relations enjoys the finite model property and has a decidable satisfiability problem. Moreover, we establish tight complexity bounds for satisfiability: TOWER-completeness in general, and $(K{+}2)$-ExpTime-completeness when the number of distinguished predicates is fixed to $K$. Finally, we show that satisfiability becomes undecidable if either the nesting condition is dropped (already with two equivalence relations) or equality is admitted (already with a single equivalence relation).

</details>


### [194] [Eliminating reversals from cubical type theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15080)
*Evan Cavallo,Christian Sattler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于自对偶区间的立方类型论，添加反转算子（交换区间端点的对合算子）是保守扩张。通过"扭转构造"（区间与其对偶的乘积仍构成带反转的区间），证明了该扩张在opaque立方类型论中不改变可证明性，并首次在无反转的立方集范畴中构造了带反转的严格立方类型论模型，其同伦论对应于拓扑空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方类型论的核心是抽象单位区间，其上可定义路径类型表示相等关系。区间上可用的不同操作对应不同的类型论。反转算子是一个对合操作，交换区间的两个端点。作者希望研究：对于自对偶区间理论（如最小两点理论或有界分配格理论），扩展反转算子（内化对偶性）是否保持原有理论的保守性。

Method: 1. 提出"扭转构造"：区间I与其对偶I^op的乘积I × I^op构成新的区间，其上反转算子由坐标交换给出。2. 利用该构造证明保守扩张性，适用于"opaque"立方类型论（无严格约简方程）。3. 在无反转的立方集范畴中，通过扭转构造构造带反转的立方类型论模型。

Result: 1. 对自对偶区间理论的扩展反转算子是保守扩张。2. 首次在无反转的立方集范畴中构造了带反转的严格立方类型论模型。3. 该模型的同伦论与拓扑空间相对应。

Conclusion: 反转算子可以保守地添加到自对偶区间的立方类型论中，且通过扭转构造可以在更基础的范畴中给出模型，确保其同伦论与拓扑空间一致。这为立方类型论中处理对称性和对偶性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Cubical type theories are designed around an abstract unit interval from which types of paths, used to represent equalities, are defined. Varying the operations available on this interval yields different type theories. A reversal is an involutive operator on the interval that swaps its two endpoints. We show that for cubical type theories with self-dual interval theories, such as the minimal theory of two endpoints or the theory of a bounded distributive lattice, the extension of the theory with a reversal that internalizes the duality is a conservative extension. The key tool is a "twist construction": the product of an interval and its dual is again an interval with a reversal given by swapping coordinates.
  Our conservativity result applies to "opaque" cubical type theories, without strict equations reducing the filling operator at concrete type formers or eliminators from higher inductive types at path constructors. Using the same twist construction, we also construct models of strict cubical type theory with reversals in categories of cubical sets without reversals. We thereby give the first model of a theory with reversals whose homotopy theory corresponds to that of topological spaces.

</details>


### [195] [Loop Termination and Generalized Collatz Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15094)
*Mishel Carelli*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究线性约束循环的终止性问题，将其与广义Collatz序列建立紧密联系。证明了一变量线性约束循环的终止性可在多项式时间内判定（假设一个长期未解决的广义Collatz猜想成立），并揭示了该问题与广义Collatz猜想之间的等价关系。此外，证明了若一变量循环存在循环轨迹，则其长度至多为2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性约束循环是程序分析中的基本模型，其终止性问题在整数、有理数和实数域上的可判定性长期以来悬而未决。本文旨在探究整数域上线性约束循环与广义Collatz序列之间的深层联系，并寻求终止性判定的可行算法。

Method: 将一变量线性约束循环的终止性问题归约到广义Collatz序列的分析框架中，利用数论和自动机理论工具建立两者之间的等价关系。通过分析循环轨迹的代数性质，给出循环轨迹长度的上界证明。

Result: （1）在广义Collatz猜想成立的前提下，一变量线性约束循环的终止性可在多项式时间内判定；（2）任何判定一变量循环终止性的过程都等价于判定广义Collatz猜想的特定实例；（3）一变量循环若有循环轨迹，其长度不超过2。

Conclusion: 本文不仅为线性约束循环终止性判定提供了新路径，还揭示了程序终止性问题与数论中未解决问题之间的深刻联系，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Linear-constraint loops are programs whose transition relation is specified by a system of linear inequalities. The termination problem asks, given a loop, whether it admits an infinite computation. Decidability of termination remains open for linear-constraint loops over integers, rationals, and reals. We focus on loops over integers and show that they are tightly connected to generalized Collatz sequences - integer sequences generated by maps that are linear on each residue class modulo a fixed natural number. We prove that termination of one-variable linear-constraint loops is decidable in polynomial time, provided a long-standing conjecture about generalized Collatz sequences holds. Conversely, we show that any decision procedure for one-variable loops would prove or refute specific instances of this conjecture, which remain open. Moreover, we show that if a one-variable loop has a cyclic trace, then it also has a cyclic trace of length at most two.

</details>


### [196] [Constructive higher sheaf models with applications to synthetic mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15126)
*Thierry Coquand,Jonas Höfer,Christian Sattler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在构造性元理论中建立了类型论的高层层模型，特别构建了单纯同伦类型论、合成代数几何和合成Stone对偶性的构造性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在合成数学领域出现了若干基于依赖类型论（扩展了单值性和高阶归纳类型）的新发展，如单纯同伦类型论、合成代数几何和合成Stone对偶性。然而这些形式系统的模型基础尚缺乏构造性元理论层面的统一支撑。

Method: 在构造性元理论框架下，为依赖类型论建立高层层（higher sheaf）模型，并利用该模型框架构造性地实现单纯同伦类型论、合成代数几何和合成Stone对偶性的具体模型。

Result: 成功在构造性元理论中构建了高层层模型，并给出了上述三种合成数学形式系统的构造性模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为合成数学的形式系统提供了构造性且统一的基础模型，证明这些系统可以在不依赖非构造性原理的情况下得到合理的语义解释。

Abstract: There have recently been several developments in synthetic mathematics using extensions of dependent type theory with univalence and higher inductive types: simplicial homotopy type theory, synthetic algebraic geometry and synthetic Stone duality. We provide a foundation of higher sheaf models of type theory in a constructive metatheory and, in particular, build constructive models of these formal systems.

</details>


### [197] [Complete Local Reasoning About Parameterized Programs Over Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15143)
*Ruotong Cheng,Azadeh Farzan*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限状态参数化并发程序在丰富通信拓扑上的算法安全验证问题，通过将问题简化为组合式方案，提出了一种自动生成全称量化归纳不变量的验证算法，并工具实现，在多种拓扑上证明了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化并发程序验证方法在处理无限状态和复杂通信拓扑时存在困难，缺乏一种通用的、完备的自动化验证方法。

Method: 在合理的拓扑假设下，将参数化族的安全验证问题约简为一组局部证明的组合式方案，通过全称量化归纳不变量实现完备性，并开发了相应的验证算法与工具。

Result: 在多种不同通信拓扑的基准测试集上，该工具能有效证明参数化程序的安全性。

Conclusion: 所提出的组合式约简方法在无限状态参数化并发程序验证上是完备且有效的，工具实现在多种拓扑上表现良好。

Abstract: This paper investigates the algorithmic safety verification problem of infinite-state parameterized concurrent programs over a rich set of communication topologies. The goal is to automatically produce a proof of correctness in the form of a universally quantified inductive invariant, where the quantification is over the nodes in the topology. We illustrate that under reasonable assumptions on the underlying topology, the problem can be reduced to and solved as a compositional scheme, that is, the verification of the parameterized family is reduced to a set of local proofs, in a complete manner. We propose a verification algorithm, which is implemented as a tool, and demonstrate through a set of benchmarks over several different topologies that our approach is effective in proving parameterized programs safe.

</details>


### [198] [Guises and Perspectives: An Intentional and Hyperintensional Sketch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15144)
*Juan J. Colomina-Alminana*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文为基于Castañeda的伪装理论（guises）构建了一套内涵逻辑系统，将伪装视为带有意向性的属性束，融合莱布尼茨包含语义、意向算子与模态层，证明了可靠性和典范模型完全性，分析了超内涵现象（如意向语境下的替代失效、准索引性、de se指称），并与Montague、Bealer、Fine、Barwise & Perry等框架进行比较，论证关系是意向性的内在结构而非外在因果联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种形式逻辑框架，将Castañeda的伪装理论（guises）形式化，从内在主义（internalist）视角理解关系，遵循莱布尼茨传统，以解决传统外延逻辑在意向语境中的局限性。

Method: 构建了包含语法、模型论和证明论的内涵逻辑系统：以伪装（作为属性束+意向）为基本语义对象；集成莱布尼茨包含语义、意向算子和模态算子（可能性和必然性定义为极大一致闭包）；通过典范模型方法建立元定理。

Result: 建立了系统的可靠性和典范模型完全性证明；成功分析了意向语境下的替代失效、准索引性和de se指称等超内涵现象；与Montague、Bealer、Fine、Barwise & Perry等经典框架进行了比较。

Conclusion: 关系不是外在的因果联系，而是意向性的内在结构，编码在伪装（guises）之中——即关系本质上是视角（perspectives）。该框架是一个自洽的形式系统，展现了莱布尼茨概念演算纲领的现代实现可能性。

Abstract: This paper develops a formal logic for guises based on the work of Héctor-Neri Castañeda, who understood relations from an internalist viewpoint, following Leibniz. We introduce a syntax, model theory, and proof theory for an intensional logic in which guises (taken as bundles of properties equipped with intention) serve as primary semantic objects. The system integrates (i) a Leibnizian containment semantics for singular truths, (ii) an intentional operator that captures internal relations among guises, and (iii) a modal layer for possibility and necessity modeled as maximally consistent closures. We establish core metatheoretic results (e.i. soundness and canonical-model completeness sketches) and analyze hyperintensional phenomena such as substitution failure in intentional contexts, quasi-indexicality, and de se reference. We compare the framework to classical intensional semantics (Montague), property theory (Bealer), hyperintensional logics (Fine), situation semantics (Barwise and Perry), and to the Leibniz program for a calculus of concepts. The result is a selfcontained formal framework that demonstrates that relations are not external causal links but intentional internal structures encoded in the guises through which agents and objects are conceived: i.e., they are perspectives.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Indian Wedding System Optimization (IWSO): A Novel Socially Inspired Metaheuristic with Operational Design and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13871)
*Deepika Saxena,Kishu Gupta,Jitendra Kumar,Jatinder Kumar,Sakshi Patni,Vinaytosh Mishra,Niharika Singh,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种受印度婚礼社会文化启发的群体元启发式优化算法IWSO，通过媒人引导策略和自适应淘汰机制，在基准函数测试中表现出优于GA、PSO、DE、CS等算法的收敛速度、解质量和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从传统印度婚礼的婚配过程中汲取灵感，通过家庭、候选人和媒人之间的协作机制，设计一种新型引导式选择性搜索框架，以解决复杂优化问题中现有方法收敛速度慢、易陷入局部最优等不足。

Method: 提出IWSO算法，包含两个核心创新：(i) 媒人引导的影响策略——精英解引导弱候选解的进化，无需外部参数即可增强收敛；(ii) 自适应淘汰与重新初始化机制——替换表现不佳的个体以维持种群多样性、防止早熟收敛。采用加权多目标适应度函数，并推导了时间与空间复杂度。

Result: 在高维和多模态基准测试函数上的大量实验表明，IWSO在收敛速度、解质量和鲁棒性方面均优于GA、PSO、DE、CS等现有优化方法。

Conclusion: IWSO作为一种受社会文化启发的新型群体元启发式算法，通过独特的媒人引导与自适应淘汰机制，在复杂优化问题上展现显著优势，为元启发式优化领域提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel population-based metaheuristic, Indian Wedding System Optimization (IWSO), inspired by the socio-cultural dynamics of traditional Indian weddings. IWSO models the matchmaking process driven by collaboration among families, candidates, and matchmakers as a guided, selective search framework for solving complex optimization problems. The algorithm introduces two key innovations: (i) a matchmaker-guided influence strategy, where elite solutions direct the evolution of weaker candidates, enhancing convergence without external parameters; and (ii) an adaptive elimination and reinitialization mechanism that maintains diversity and prevents premature convergence by replacing underperforming individuals. IWSO employs a weighted multi-objective fitness function and analytically derived time and space complexity, benchmarked against existing optimization approaches such as Genetic Algorithm (GA), Partical Swarm Optimization (PSO), Differential Evolution (DE), Cuckoo Search (CS), etc. Extensive experiments on benchmark high-dimensional and multimodal test functions demonstrate superior performance of IWSO in terms of convergence speed, solution quality, and robustness.

</details>


### [200] [S-AI-Recursive: A Bio-Inspired and Temporal Sparse AI Architecture for Iterative, Introspective, and Energy-Frugal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13872)
*Said Slaoui*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: S-AI-Recursive是一个受生物启发的稀疏人工智能架构，通过激素驱动的递归推理循环（RRC）实现认知迭代，在不到一千万参数下达到竞争性推理性能，证明了“时间简约性”原则：迭代认知深度可替代架构宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统前馈架构将推理视为单次前向传递，缺乏生物认知中的迭代反馈机制。本文旨在将推理操作化为类似生物激素调控的闭环迭代过程，以更少的参数实现更强的推理能力。

Method: 形式化了递归推理循环（RRC）作为动力系统，由两种新激素Clarifine（收敛信号）和Confusionin（不确定性检测器）拮抗调控；建立了完整的数学框架，包括递归状态动力学、Lyapunov稳定性证明、熵收缩定理、激素停止准则（有限时间终止保证）、Euler-Maruyama离散化与投影、原始-对偶智能体选择及递归印记记忆预热加速。

Result: 在SAI-UT+测试基准上，S-AI-Recursive以不到一千万参数在抽象和符号推理基准上达到竞争性推理性能。

Conclusion: 验证了“时间简约性”核心原则：迭代认知深度可以替代架构宽度，即在小型架构中通过递归迭代实现深度推理能力。

Abstract: This article introduces S-AI-Recursive, a bio-inspired Sparse Artificial Intelligence architecture in which reasoning is operationalized as a hormonal closed-loop iteration rather than a single feed-forward pass. Building upon the S-AI foundational framework [1], the hormonal-probabilistic unification doctrine [2], and the formal mathematical methodology established in S-AI-IoT [3], the present work formalizes the Recursive Reasoning Cycle (RRC) as a dynamical system governed by two novel hormones: Clarifine, a convergence signal, and Confusionin, an uncertainty detector, whose antagonistic regulation drives iterative state refinement toward a stable cognitive equilibrium. The complete mathematical framework is developed, including recursive state dynamics, Lyapunov stability proof, entropic contraction theorem, hormonal stopping criterion with finite-time termination guarantee, Euler-Maruyama discretization with projection, primal-dual agent selection under iteration budget, and recursive engram memory with warm-start acceleration. Experimental validation on the SAI-UT+ testbench demonstrates that S-AI-Recursive achieves competitive reasoning performance on abstract and symbolic benchmarks with fewer than ten million parameters, confirming the central principle of temporal parsimony: iterative cognitive depth substitutes for architectural width.

</details>


### [201] [GEAR: Genetic AutoResearch for Agentic Code Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13874)
*Ahmadreza Jeddi,Minh Ngoc Le,Hakki C. Karaimer,Konstantinos G. Derpanis,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: GEAR是一种基于种群搜索的自主研究框架，通过维护多个研究状态、使用遗传算子（选择、变异、交叉）替代传统单路径搜索，在相同计算预算下显著优于基线方法，并能持续发现改进而非陷入局部最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究智能体依赖单一路径搜索策略（反复修改单一程序，只保留改进当前最优结果的变化），导致丢弃有用的局部想法、替代性有前景的方向以及失败或不完整实验中的洞见。

Method: GEAR采用种群搜索方法：维护一组强候选解，基于生产力、新颖性和覆盖率选择父代，通过变异和交叉探索新想法。每个研究状态存储代码变更、反思和性能数据。研究实现了三个版本——提示控制版、固定程序化搜索控制器版、控制器可自进化版。

Result: 在相同计算预算和环境下，三个版本均优于AutoResearch基线。基线倾向于陷入局部最优，而GEAR在长时间运行中持续发现改进。

Conclusion: 自主研究智能体在维护多个有前景的方向并能够随时间调整搜索策略时效率更高。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can already run machine learning experiments without human supervision, but many rely on a narrow search strategy: they repeatedly modify one program and keep changes only when they improve the current best result. This can cause them to discard useful partial ideas, alternative promising directions, and insights from failed or incomplete experiments. GEAR, or Genetic AutoResearch, replaces this single-path search with a population-based search over multiple research states. It keeps a set of strong candidate solutions, selects parents based on productivity, novelty, and coverage, and explores new ideas through mutation and crossover. Each research state stores its code changes, reflections, and performance data, allowing future decisions to build on past discoveries. The paper studies three versions of GEAR: one controlled through prompting, one using a fixed programmatic search controller, and one where the controller itself can evolve during the run. Under the same compute budget and environment, all three versions outperform the AutoResearch baseline. More importantly, while the baseline tends to settle into one local optimum, GEAR continues finding improvements over longer runs. Overall, the results suggest that autonomous research agents become more effective when they maintain multiple promising directions and can adapt their search strategy over time.

</details>


### [202] [ARES-LSHADE: Autoresearch-Enhanced LSHADE with Memetic Polish for the GNBG Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13877)
*Abdullah Naeem,Md Wasi Ul kabir,Manish Bhatt,Ayon Dey,Anav Katwal,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARES-LSHADE，一种基于记忆差分进化的模因算法，在GECCO 2026 LLM设计进化算法竞赛中针对GNBG基准测试表现了出色性能。算法在LLM-LSHADE 2025获胜者基础上新增了侦察增强变异算子与自适应CMA-ES集成（通过约30次LLM驱动实验获得）以及多启动L-BFGS-B精化阶段。在24个函数中，ARES-LSHADE获得了744场胜利中的510场（差值<1e-8），在18个函数上达到机器精度。论文还报告了两个方法论观察：LLM研究循环在仅操作符编辑面和仅适应度观测空间下收敛到特征平台；拓宽观测空间包含基准元数据后，算法虽能解决所有24个函数但违反黑箱规则。讨论了LLM能力与基准完整性之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参赛GECCO 2026 LLM设计进化算法竞赛，基于LLM-LSHADE 2025获胜者进一步改进，探索LLM驱动的算法自动设计能力，同时检验LLM设计算法在严格黑箱基准上的极限与边界。

Method: (a) 侦察增强变异算子与自适应CMA-ES集成：通过约30次LLM驱动的自主研究实验自动设计；(b) 多启动L-BFGS-B精化阶段：在严格黑箱条件下对最终种群进行局部优化。

Result: 在官方31次运行评估中，ARES-LSHADE获得510/744场胜利（每函数差距<1e-8），在24个函数中的18个达到机器精度；剩余6个函数表现出与GNBG组合结构一致的特征平台现象。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的研究循环在受限观测空间下会收敛到特征平台；拓宽观测空间（如包含基准元数据）可大幅提升性能但可能违反黑箱规则。这为未来LLM驱动的优化算法研究提供了设计考量。

Abstract: We present ARES-LSHADE, a memetic differential-evolution variant submitted to the GECCO 2026 competition on LLM-designed evolutionary algorithms for the Generalized Numerical Benchmark Generator (GNBG). The algorithm builds on the LLM-LSHADE 2025 winner, contributing two new components: (a) a scout-augmented mutation operator with adaptive CMA-ES integration, produced by an autonomous research loop across approximately thirty LLM-driven design experiments, and (b) a multi-start L-BFGS-B polish phase that respects strict blackbox treatment of the benchmark. On the official 31-run-per-function evaluation with the competition-specified function-evaluation budgets, ARES-LSHADE obtains 510 of 744 wins (per-function gap below 1e-8), reaching machine precision on 18 of 24 functions. The remaining six functions exhibit characteristic plateau signatures consistent with GNBG's compositional structure, and were independently identified by the autoresearch loop as the hardest of the suite. Beyond the result itself, this report documents two methodological observations: (i) an LLM-driven research loop with operator-only edit surface and fitness-only observation space converges to a characteristic plateau on this benchmark; (ii) when we initially widened the observation space to include the benchmark's compositional metadata, the resulting algorithm trivially solved all 24 functions but violated the competition's blackbox rule, which we identified before submission. We discuss this tension between LLM capability and benchmark integrity as a design consideration for future LLM-driven optimization-algorithm research. Code and reproducibility artifacts are available at https://github.com/anaeem1/ARES-LSHADE.

</details>


### [203] [Darwin Family: MRI-Trust-Weighted Evolutionary Merging for Training-Free Scaling of Language-Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14386)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Junghoon Shin,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Darwin Family提出了一种无需训练的进化式合并框架，通过梯度无关的权重空间重组来提升大语言模型的推理能力。旗舰模型Darwin-27B-Opus在GPQA Diamond上达到86.9%，在1252个模型中排名第6，超越了其全训练的基础模型。该方法支持跨架构育种、递归多代进化，无需任何基于梯度的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以在不进行额外训练的情况下，通过重组已有检查点中编码的潜在能力来提升前沿级别的推理性能，从而替代成本高昂的后训练流水线。

Method: 提出三个核心思想：(i) 14维自适应合并基因组，实现细粒度的组件级和块级重组；(ii) MRI-Trust Fusion，通过可学习的信任参数自适应平衡诊断性层重要性信号与进化搜索；(iii) Architecture Mapper，支持异构模型族之间的跨架构育种。

Result: Darwin-27B-Opus在GPQA Diamond上达到86.9%，在1252个评估模型中排名第6，超越其全训练的基础模型。在4B到35B参数规模上，Darwin模型均持续优于其父模型，支持递归多代进化，并能合并Transformer和Mamba架构组件。

Conclusion: 诊断引导的进化式合并是成本高昂的后训练流水线的一种实用且可重复的替代方案，适用于以推理为中心的语言模型。

Abstract: We present Darwin Family, a framework for training-free evolutionary merging of large language models via gradient-free weight-space recombination. We ask whether frontier-level reasoning performance can be improved without additional training, by reorganizing latent capabilities already encoded in existing checkpoints. Darwin introduces three key ideas: (i) a 14-dimensional adaptive merge genome enabling fine-grained component- and block-level recombination; (ii) MRI-Trust Fusion, which adaptively balances diagnostic layer-importance signals with evolutionary search through a learnable trust parameter; and (iii) an Architecture Mapper that enables cross-architecture breeding between heterogeneous model families. Empirically, the flagship Darwin-27B-Opus achieves 86.9% on GPQA Diamond, ranking #6 among 1,252 evaluated models, and outperforming its fully trained foundation model without any gradient-based training. Across scales from 4B to 35B parameters, Darwin models consistently improve over their parents, support recursive multi-generation evolution, and enable a training-free evolutionary merge that combines Transformer- and Mamba-based components. Together, the Darwin Family demonstrates that diagnostic-guided evolutionary merging is a practical and reproducible alternative to costly post-training pipelines for reasoning-centric language models.

</details>


### [204] [First Mathematical Runtime Analyses of Multi-Objective Evolutionary Algorithms for Multi-Valued Decision Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14836)
*Mingfeng Li,Zheng Cheng,Weijie Zheng,Benjamin Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次对处理多值（r>2）决策变量的多目标进化算法（MOEA）进行数学运行时间分析。针对经典SEMO算法在r值OneMinMax基准问题上的表现，证明了期望函数评估次数的上界O(n²r² log n)和下界Ω(n²r (r + log n))，并通过仅接受严格改进解的变体算法将上下界匹配为O(n²r (r + log n))。结果表明经典MOEA处理多值变量时无显著额外困难，但更复杂的算法需要更高级的分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多目标进化算法在二元决策空间问题上的理论分析已相当深入，但实际中大量问题涉及多值（r>2）决策变量，而此前对此类问题缺乏严格的数学运行时间分析，存在研究空白。

Method: 以经典SEMO算法为研究对象，采用单位强度局部变异算子，将标准OneMinMax基准问题推广至r值版本进行理论分析。推导出期望运行时间的上下界，并引入仅接受严格改进解的算法变体以缩小上下界差距。

Result: 标准SEMO算法的上界为O(n²r² log n)，下界为Ω(n²r (r + log n))；仅接受严格改进解的变体算法上界为O(n²r (r + log n))，与下界匹配。

Conclusion: 经典MOEA在处理多值决策变量时不会遇到显著的额外困难；但对于具有更复杂种群动态的算法，需要更高级的分析工具才能获得紧界。

Abstract: Problems defined on binary decision spaces have been intensively studied in the theory of multi-objective evolutionary algorithms (MOEAs). In contrast, no mathematical runtime analyses exist so far for MOEAs dealing with decision variables that take a finite number $r > 2$ of values, despite the prevalence of such problems in practice. In this work, we begin to fill this research gap. We analyze how the classic SEMO algorithm with unit-strength local mutation computes the Pareto front of an $r$-valued counterpart of the classic \oneminmax benchmark. For the expected number of function evaluations until the Pareto front is covered by the population of this MOEA, we prove an upper bound of $O(n^2 r^2 \log n)$ and a near-tight lower bound of $Ω(n^2 r (r + \log n))$. We can close the small remaining gap between these two bounds by considering a variant of the algorithm that accepts only strictly better solutions; for this variant, we show an upper bound of $O(n^2 r (r + \log n))$, matching our lower bound (which also holds for this variant). Our results suggest that classic MOEAs encounter no significant additional difficulties when dealing with multi-valued decision variables. However, significantly more advanced tools may be required to obtain tight bounds for algorithms with more complex population dynamics.

</details>


### [205] [BiSpikCLM: A Spiking Language Model integrating Softmax-Free Spiking Attention and Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13859)
*Sihang Guo,Chenlin Zhou,Jiaqi Wang,Kehai Chen,Qingyan Meng,Zhengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出BiSpikCLM，首个全二值脉冲因果语言模型，通过软无Softmax脉冲注意力（SFSA）和尖峰感知对齐蒸馏（SpAD），在仅需少量训练token的情况下实现与ANN相当的性能，计算成本仅为后者的4.16%-5.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲大语言模型仍依赖浮点矩阵乘法和非线性操作，或面临复杂的时空动态训练困难，缺乏高效、低功耗的脉冲驱动语言模型方案。

Method: 提出Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA)消除softmax和浮点运算；引入Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD)在嵌入、注意力图、中间特征和输出logits上对齐ANN教师和SNN学生。

Result: BiSpikCLM在自然语言生成任务上仅需ANN 5.6%的训练token即可达到可比性能，计算成本仅为ANN的4.16%-5.87%。

Conclusion: 全二值脉冲驱动的大语言模型具有可行性和有效性，蒸馏方法为脑启发脉冲自然语言处理提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer promising energy-efficient alternatives to large language models (LLMs) due to their event-driven nature and ultra-low power consumption. However, to preserve capacity, most existing spiking LLMs still incur intensive floating-point matrix multiplication (MatMul) and nonlinearities, or training difficulties arising from the complex spatiotemporal dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose BiSpikCLM, the first fully binary spiking MatMul-free causal language model. BiSpikCLM introduces Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA), eliminating softmax and floating-point operations in autoregressive language modeling. For efficient training, we introduce Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD), which aligns ANN teacher and SNN student across embeddings, attention maps, intermediate features, and output logits. SpAD framework allows BiSpikCLM to reach comparable performance to ANN counterparts using substantially fewer training tokens (e.g., only 5.6% of the tokens for the 1.3B model). As a result, BiSpikCLM achieves competitive performance at only 4.16% - 5.87% of the computational cost on natural language generation tasks. Our results highlight the feasibility and effectiveness of fully binary spike-driven LLMs and establish the distillation as a promising pathway for brain-inspired spiking NLP.

</details>
